<a name="prompt-2098411323562324348"></a>

### A 30-second 3:4 vertical shot-by-shot prompt for a beautiful woman's outfit transformation, featuring card-based character selection, action-matched cuts \(yellow/orange/green/pink outfits\), doll showcases, and comic collage posters.

Author：[@johnAGI168](https://x.com/johnAGI168) · [Source](https://x.com/johnAGI168/status/2098411323562324348)

Comic / Storyboard · Poster / Flyer · Cinematic / Film Still · Comic / Graphic Novel · Character · Fashion Item · Published

**Summary:** A 30-second 3:4 vertical shot-by-shot prompt for a beautiful woman's outfit transformation, featuring card-based character selection, action-matched cuts \(yellow/orange/green/pink outfits\), doll showcases, and comic collage posters.

<img src="covers/2098411323562324348.jpg" alt="Image 1" width="480" />

**Prompt**

```text
Duration: 30 seconds
Aspect Ratio: 3:4 vertical screen
A live-action themed outfit transformation short film combining character selection cards, match-cut action transitions, and comic photo collages. Fast-paced, playful, and lively.

[Character Reference]
Image 1 serves as the sole character identity reference.
The entire film consistently features the same adult female, with stable facial identity and body proportions.
The opening uses the original outfit and original hairstyle from the character card.
The subsequent four outfits and the hairstyles following completed transformations must follow the instructions below.
Before each complete look appears, retain the opening hairstyle first, switching hairstyles, accessories, and makeup styling via the designated action.
Do not bring the background or multi-view layout of the character card into the video.

[Overall Video Structure]
Four silhouette cards are presented at the opening for selection.
The presentation order is strictly fixed as: Yellow → Orange → Green → Pink.
Each outfit follows this flow:
Silhouette card flips into a full-color character card → New outfit with the opening hairstyle → Designated action triggers complete transformation → Showcase holding the corresponding doll → Three-photo comic collage.
Finally, a sequential recap of all four looks.

[0–3s: Character Selection]
Light gray studio backdrop; the character turns from a semi-profile toward the camera.
Both hands frame a rectangular viewfinder in front of her chest using her thumbs and index fingers.

Four floating vertical cards fan out below her chest.
Strictly from screen left to right: Orange, Pink, Green, Yellow.
In the center of each card face is a distinct, cute black doll silhouette.

The character taps her right index finger toward the cards, then performs a selection gesture as if grabbing an invisible thread and pulling it back toward her body.
The rightmost yellow card is selected and scales forward.
The card flips, revealing the black silhouette as a full-color yellow doll character, then slides away to reveal the subject.
The cards are an independent animation layer; no physical grasp of the card with fingers is required.

[3–8s: Yellow Look, Raising Camera Triggers Transformation]
Light blue background, accented with yellow stars and comic-style small burst lines.

Outfit consists of a yellow short-sleeved collared shirt, dark blue bowtie, and dark blue pleated mini-skirt with star details.
The character initially retains the opening hairstyle, holding a yellow Polaroid camera with both hands, its yellow camera strap hanging naturally.

The character raises the camera from her chest to eye level, partially obscuring her face, making a photo-taking gesture.
A clean match cut occurs right on the beat when the camera covers the face.
The next shot reveals the completed look: neatly groomed hair styled with yellow star hairclips; hands now hold a small yellow doll.
The prop swap is achieved entirely through editing; the camera does not melt or morph into the doll on-screen.

The character brings the doll close to the side of her face, rests her other hand on her hip, and tilts her head with a smile.

Approximately in the final 1 second, transitions into a yellow comic collage:
A thick white outline first appears along the character's contour, then the cut-out figure is scaled and arranged into a poster layout.
A large full-body photo sits in the foreground, with two rectangular frames in the background displaying close-up photos.
All three photos feature the same person in the exact same yellow look.
The character photos remain completely static; yellow halftone dots, diagonal frames, and small doll stickers may feature subtle motion.

An orange silhouette card pops into the foreground, flips to reveal an orange fox doll, then slides away into the next outfit.

[8–14s: Orange Look, Back-Turn Head Whip Transformation]
Cream-yellow background with orange diagonally sliced border graphics.

Orange bandeau tube top paired with an orange-and-white tiered ruffled mini-skirt.
Initially retaining the opening hairstyle, the character gently holds the skirt hem, swaying her body to the beat.

The character turns her back to the camera, then swiftly whips her head and hair back around.
A match-cut transition occurs right at the moment the hair whips and obscures the face.
Turning back to face front, the hairstyle has transformed into two long black braids, accessorized with orange-and-white fox ear headpieces and star ornaments.
Facial identity remains consistent; the long braids naturally swing with the inertia of the turn.

Briefly showcases the full look and skirt hem, then cuts to a pose holding the orange fox doll.
With the doll held close beside her cheek, the character closes her eyes with a gentle smile.

Approximately in the final 1 second, transitions into an orange comic collage:
In the foreground is a playful full-body cut-out lifting one foot, with two close-up freeze-frame photos in the background.
Thick white outlines, orange halftone dots, and burst lines compose the layout.
Character photos remain static, while background graphics retain subtle animation.

A green silhouette card pops into the foreground, flips into a green doll character, then slides away.

[14–19s: Green Look, Crown Toss Transformation]
Mint green background.

Light green corset-style top with yellow lacing and off-the-shoulder accents, paired with a pale yellow sheer tulle maxi skirt.
Initially retaining the opening hairstyle, the character balances a small golden crown on the palm of her right hand, presenting it in front of her chest.

The character tosses the crown gently upward, her palm following through in an upward sweep.
A match cut occurs on the beat as the crown ascends and the character turns slightly sideways.
In the next shot, the character is already wearing the same crown, with her hairstyle transformed into long, voluminous reddish-brown curls.
The crown's placement is precise and stable; no duplicate crowns appear, and the crown does not clip through the head.

Maintaining her semi-profile stance, she completes the follow-through motion of her hand, then shifts her gaze toward the camera.
Cuts to a pose cradling a red rose in one arm and lifting the green doll close beside her face with the other hand.

Approximately in the final 1 second, transitions into a green comic collage:
Foreground displays a static full-body cut-out in the long skirt, with two close-up photos behind.
Green borders, halftone dots, small crown, and doll stickers maintain a comic aesthetic.
Expressions and hair within the photos remain frozen, while the graphic layer as a whole can slide in.

A pink silhouette card pops up, flips into a pink doll character, then slides away.

[19–24s: Pink & Blue Look, Racket Swing Transformation]
Light purple background, pink diagonal graphic cuts, accented with minimal tennis racket line art.

Pink and mint blue patterned sleeveless top, light blue denim pleated mini-skirt, pink belt.
Initially retaining the opening hairstyle, the character grips a white tennis racket with both hands, executing a single horizontal swing.

A match cut occurs right on the beat when the racket and arms swiftly sweep across the front of her body.
As the swing concludes, the styling has transformed into twin side buns styled with small ribbons and hairpins.
The swing trajectory and body posture remain continuous; no repeated second swing, without using the racket to cover the entire screen.

The character completes the racket follow-through pose, tilting her head to smile at the camera.
Then cuts to a medium close-up holding the pink doll beside her face, the other hand resting naturally on her hip.

Approximately in the final 1 second, transitions into a pink comic collage:
One foreground full-body cut-out alongside two background close-up photos.
All three character photos remain frozen with clean white outlines.
Background color blocks, halftone patterns, and doll stickers can exhibit subtle motion.

[24–30s: Four-Look Recap]
Approximately 1.5 seconds per look, hard cut precisely on beat in the order: Yellow, Orange, Green, Pink & Blue.
Background colors match each respective look, with uniform medium close-up framing.

Yellow:
Holds the yellow doll in one hand, pointing clearly at the doll with the other index finger, displaying a playful expression.

Orange:
Holds the orange doll in one hand, bringing a small fist close to her cheek with the other hand, giving a single wink.

Green:
Holds the green doll close to her face, tilting her head gently, closing her eyes with a tender smile.

Pink & Blue:
Holds the pink doll in front of her chest near her chin with both hands, giving a brief winking expression.
Finally holds this pose, concluding on the final musical downbeat.

[Comic Collages & Cards]
Cards must include a reveal process from black silhouette to colored doll; cannot simply be color block obscurations.
Comic posters for all four looks follow a uniform layout: one full-body subject, two close-up photo frames, white outlines, corresponding colored halftone dots, and doll stickers.
Character photos must be genuinely frozen; no continued blinking, talking, or hair movement.
Background graphics and overall photo layers may animate.
Multiple figures in the collage are photos of the same individual, not multiple real people appearing simultaneously in the studio.

[Music & Sound]
Upbeat, brisk pop soundtrack with clear drum beats throughout the entire film.
Raising the camera, whipping hair, tossing the crown, and swinging the racket hit distinct musical downbeats respectively.
Photo freezes paired with a crisp shutter click, card flips paired with a snappy card-flip sound.
No character dialogue, no lip-syncing/mouth movement for speaking, no dialogue subtitles.

[Visual Constraints]
Do not add brand logos, random commercial text, or garbled characters; preserve character cards, color blocks, doll stickers, and comic embellishments.
Do not alter the character's facial identity; do not introduce new outfits.
Colors and shapes of each doll remain consistent.
No fused fingers, extraneous limbs, or clipping props.
Outfit transformations rely on action match-cutting; do not use body melting, morphing vortexes, or smoke obscuration.
```

<a name="prompt-2098353311808385384"></a>

### Storyboard prompt for a cinematic stunt shot of a race car jumping through an overturning train carriage and drifting to safety.

Author：[@TanLuAI](https://x.com/TanLuAI) · [Source](https://x.com/TanLuAI/status/2098353311808385384)

Comic / Storyboard · Cinematic / Film Still · Vehicle · Published

Source：[@TanLuAI](https://x.com/TanLuAI) · [Source](https://x.com/TanLuAI/status/2098041885856272478)

**Summary:** Storyboard prompt for a cinematic stunt shot of a race car jumping through an overturning train carriage and drifting to safety.

<img src="covers/2098353311808385384.jpg" alt="Image 1" width="480" />

**Prompt**

```text
[Generation Requirements] Generate a 10-second, 16:9 widescreen, live-action cinematic car stunt video. The visuals must possess intense impact, tight pacing, and clear physical feedback. A white-and-red supercar charges at high speed toward an overturned teal train carriage, leans sideways off an incline ramp, crashes in through a large side window, traverses across the carriage interior, and shatters the opposite glass window to burst out. The moment the car lands, the train carriage slams violently down behind it; the car enters a continuous drift and successfully escapes danger. Except for a newly added 2-second slow-motion shot of breaking through the window, all other shots maintain high speed. The car continually moves forward, with no stopping, prepping to launch, or repositioning. [Asset Anchors and Reference Rules] Reference video Sept 9: Camera work, trajectory, spatial relationships, and pacing reference. The low-slung geometric vehicle with white and red stripes corresponds to the race car in Image 1; the teal horizontal rectangular prism corresponds to the train carriage in Image 2; the tilted geometric plate corresponds to the metal ramp. The yellow window frame only marks the traversal position and must ultimately be replaced with a realistic silver metallic window frame. Use only 8 seconds from the reference video; insert a 2-second close-up side-angle slow-motion shot at the moment the car is about to smash through the exit glass, with subsequent shots delayed accordingly, totaling 10 seconds for the final video. Retain the camera positions, shot scales, vehicle path, carriage overturn direction, and movement speed of the corresponding segments. On this basis, add realistic suspension, tires, shattering, collision, and environmental feedback. The newly added slow motion seamlessly connects to the same window-exit action without repeating the impact. Image 1: Car appearance. Low-profile, wide-body supercar in white, with red longitudinal racing stripes and red side accents, black tinted cockpit glass, slender headlights, dark wheel rims, red brake calipers, and a carbon-fiber lower body kit. Vehicle model, proportions, and livery remain consistent throughout the film. Minor fine scratches are permitted after smashing through the window, while the bodywork remains intact. Image 2: Carriage appearance. Deep teal modern urban train carriage with silver metallic window frames, large side windows, light-colored interior, seats, handrails, ceiling light strips, and bottom bogies. The interior is empty of people, with the central large windows on both sides directly facing each other, keeping the horizontal pass-through zone clear. The studio background from the reference image must not appear in the video. [Visual Style] Realistic car stunt cinematography, real-world proportions, natural lighting, delicate car paint, and weighty mechanical texture. Bright overcast day after rain, with sunlight breaking through clouds to illuminate flying glass shards and water droplets; the city is overall cool gray, making the white-and-red car and teal carriage stand out vividly. Enhance the motion depth across foreground, midground, and background: nearby debris and guardrails whip past at high speed, the car in the midground remains sharp, and distant buildings establish a stable spatial reference. Normal-speed shots use natural directional motion blur; in slow motion, glass facets, water droplets, and vehicle reflections are distinctly discernible. Impact is conveyed through body deformation, suspension strain, debris directionality, road surface feedback, and brief camera shakes. The camera consistently tracks key actions, quickly stabilizing after impact tremors. [Scene] A closed elevated highway in a modern Tokyo urban district, adjacent to elevated train tracks. Dark gray asphalt is covered with a thin film of water after rain, with scattered shallow puddles reflecting vehicles and sky. The ground features worn lane markings, drainage grates, expansion joints, and fine gravel. Concrete barriers, metal sound barriers, streetlights, and curved reflective markers are sequentially arranged along both sides, providing high-speed parallax. The wide left-hand curve ahead remains clear, visibly revealing the car's turn entry, tail slide, and grip recovery line. Steel girders, bridge piers, railway ties, and metal components of the adjacent track are visible. The carriage overturns horizontally toward the road, exposing bottom mechanical structures, with partially loosened parts vibrating with inertia. Interior light strips, handrails, and window frames rotate with the carriage body, enhancing spatial dynamics. Densely packed residential towers, glass office buildings, and rooftop equipment extend layer upon layer into the distance, with a light perspective haze in the post-rain air. Background architecture does not obstruct the car, window openings, or escape route. No pedestrians are on the road or inside the carriage. [Shot Content, Camera Movement, and Sound Effects] [00:00—00:01.21] Shot 1: Colossal Object Overturns, Car Intrudes. Low-angle shot facing forward. The opening shows only the teal carriage flipping sideways toward the road and the camera, its body horizontally blocking the path ahead. As it rolls, the lower steel chassis and bogies are exposed, window reflections rapidly shift, loose metal components rattle, and a few old glass fragments and accumulated water fling off the carriage body. The white-and-red race car then storms in at high speed from behind the camera, swiftly dominating the foreground; its tires slice through the surface water film, throwing out two low plumes of water spray behind it. The camera cuts to a tracking follow-shot as the massive carriage rapidly approaches ahead of the car. Sound effects: Heavy metal twisting, continuous structural groaning and vibrations, the roar of the engine sweeping in violently from far to near, accompanied by tire water dispersion and air pressure noise. [00:01.21—00:02.29] Shot 2: Launching off the Ramp, Skimming the Window Frame. Low-angle rear-quarter high-speed tracking shot. The car's right-side wheels hit the metal ramp in rapid succession; the ramp instantly vibrates under pressure, bouncing up small amounts of gravel and water droplets from its edges. The right suspension compresses first, then rebounds as the wheels leave the ramp, raising the right side of the chassis, tilting the entire car to the left, and becoming briefly airborne. The front of the car aligns precisely with the rotating side window. The clearance between the window frame edge and the car roof rapidly narrows, tires still spinning at high speed, with the undercarriage shadow swiftly sweeping across the asphalt, demonstrating unmistakable forward inertia. Sound effects: Two rapid, heavy clanks on the ramp, chassis vibration, high-revving engine scream, and a sudden surge of wind resistance. [00:02.29—00:03.29] Shot 3: Shattering the Entry, Piercing the Confined Space. The camera closely follows the car as its nose slams into the large entry window. Glass at the contact point fractures abruptly, then explodes inward into the carriage interior, generating a stream of debris dispersing along the car's forward path. The car bursts through the opening, glass granules peppering seats and handrails, with a few shards whipping past the edges of the frame. Bright outdoor ambient light instantly cuts to brief interior carriage lighting, while overhead light strips and vertical poles blur past at high speed, heightening the sensation of speed inside the confined space. The opposite exit window remains intact, with the road outside clearly visible. The car's front closes in rapidly, cutting just before contacting the exit glass. Sound effects: Sharp shattering blast, dense metallic clatter of shards striking metal and interior fixtures, and a brief, echoing engine roar inside the carriage. [00:03.29—00:05.29] Shot 4: Side Close-up, Glass Exploding Outward from the Frame. Cut to an oblique side-angle close-up outside the exit window, keeping the teal carriage exterior, silver window frame, and the approaching white-and-red car in frame. Enters 2 seconds of high-speed slow motion, seamlessly continuing the same window-exit action. The front of the car strikes the glass; dense fracture lines spread instantly across adjacent panes. Immediately, the glass is shoved outward by the car's nose, bursting into a swarm of fine particles and clusters of partially intact fragments, fanning out layer by layer along the forward momentum. **The glass possesses three clear layers of depth:** a few foreground shards streak past the camera lens; midground shards surround the nose and front wheels, flashing cool white glints as they tumble; background glass continues peeling away from the frame edges. Gaps between fragments keep the white-and-red chassis continuously visible. The front bumper, front wheels, cockpit, doors, and rear wheels emerge in sequence. The camera tracks horizontally over a short distance alongside the body, as the red livery glides through sparkling fragments. Water droplets are flung off the car's surface, glass granules graze the paint, the window frame visibly buckles, and a brief burst of sparks flares at the abrasion point. The carriage continues to roll while the car keeps thrusting outward. The rear wheels fully clear the frame, glass shards still expanding behind, before returning to normal speed. [00:05.29—00:06.50] Shot 5: Hard Landing, Carriage Slams Down with a Crash. Cut to a low-angle shot in front and to the side of the car. The car levels out, tires pounding the road in sequence; the suspension noticeably compresses, the body dips and quickly rebounds. Tires press out flat sheets of spray, the undercarriage briefly skims close to the wet asphalt, and the vehicle continues lunging forward. Immediately after, the side of the train carriage behind crashes onto the road. The contact edge deforms first under localized crushing before the heavy mass slams down as a whole, buckling the outer shell with dents, rattling window frames, and dislodging remaining glass in clusters. The impact kicks up a low mist of water, fine gravel, and concrete dust across the road. The carriage slides a short distance along the pavement, dragging bright sparks along the friction line as loose components rattle down. ### Create a 30-second realistic cinematic suspense short film depicting a covert confrontation and pursuit among three people in an aging apartment elevator and corridor.
```

Author：[@bmx\_ai13](https://x.com/bmx_ai13) · [Source](https://x.com/bmx_ai13/status/2097961776969109709)

Photography · Cinematic / Film Still · Architecture / Interior · Published

**Summary:** Create a 30-second realistic cinematic suspense short film depicting a covert confrontation and pursuit among three people in an aging apartment elevator and corridor.

<img src="covers/2097961776969109709.jpg" alt="Image 1" width="480" />

**Prompt**

```text
Create a 30-second photorealistic cinematic suspense thriller in 16:9 widescreen. Premium theatrical cinematography, grounded performances, carefully motivated camera movement, believable physical action, and tightly controlled tension.

SETTING AND CHARACTERS:
An aging Hong Kong apartment building: cramped elevator with honey-colored wood panels, scratched brass fittings, and warm overhead lighting; outside, narrow corridors with green-and-ochre tiles, metal security gates, exposed pipes, and cold fluorescent lights.
Maintain three consistent characters throughout:
— A young adult East Asian woman with a straight black bob, red-and-gray plaid overshirt, white over-ear headphones, and a black crossbody bag.
— A middle-aged East Asian protector with short dark hair, rectangular glasses, charcoal utility vest, and dark polo shirt. His apparently ordinary manner conceals constant vigilance.
— An elderly East Asian pursuer with a weathered face, brown flat cap, round glasses, dark cardigan, and patterned neck scarf. Almost motionless, quietly threatening.

00:00–00:04 — THE HIDDEN THREAT
Begin immediately on an extreme close-up of the elderly man’s hand concealing a closed folding knife beside his cardigan. A restrained metallic click. Cut to a wide interior elevator composition: the woman stands foreground left wearing headphones; the elderly man waits behind her, right. Amber light, oppressive stillness. Slowly push forward as the elevator doors begin closing.
00:04–00:08 — THE INTERRUPTION
A hand catches the closing doors. They reopen, revealing the bespectacled protector against the cold green corridor. He enters calmly and positions himself between the woman and the elderly man. In a close two-shot, he gestures for her to lower her headphones. She does, initially irritated, then notices his serious expression. His eyes briefly track the concealed hand.
00:08–00:12 — THE SILENT CONFRONTATION
Controlled lateral camera movement reveals all three faces in layered depth. The protector casually offers a red apple, using the gesture to turn toward the pursuer. Cut to the elderly man’s eyes beneath his cap; then his fingers tightening around the concealed knife. The woman watches their exchange, beginning to understand. Restrained acting, no exaggerated reactions.
00:12–00:15 — SOMEONE IS WATCHING
Hard cut to a dim surveillance room illuminated by cool blue monitors. A headset-wearing operator leans toward a screen showing the elderly man inside the same elevator. Push toward the monitor, then match-cut from his surveillance image to his face in the elevator. The low musical pulse accelerates.

00:15–00:20 — THE EXIT
An elevator chime interrupts the tension. Doors open onto the green-tiled corridor. The protector guides the woman out with a small, urgent gesture while maintaining an ordinary expression. Track backward ahead of them. Behind their shoulders, the elderly man remains centered inside the amber elevator, staring. The doors begin closing around him.
00:20–00:25 — THE LOCK
Follow the pair along the narrow apartment walkway in one smooth tracking shot. They stop at a worn metal apartment door. Tight insert: the protector tries a key; it catches without turning. His composure slips for the first time. The woman looks back down the corridor. Rack focus from her anxious profile to the distant elevator as its doors reopen.

00:25–00:30 — THE CLIFFHANGER
The elderly pursuer steps into the corridor, now closer than expected. Low, measured footsteps. Intercut once with the protector finally turning the key. The apartment door opens and he ushers the woman inside. From inside the apartment, the camera holds on the narrowing doorway as the pursuer approaches. The door slams just before he reaches it. Cut to black at 00:29. Hold black for the final second with one quiet metallic click.
VISUAL DIRECTION:
Compose natively for 16:9, using the horizontal space for foreground obstruction, threatening background figures, and clear character relationships. Natural skin texture, detailed fabric, subtle film grain, rich blacks with visible shadow detail. Warm amber elevator light contrasted with cool green corridors and blue surveillance screens. Use 35mm framing for spatial tension, 85mm close-ups for eyes and hands. Realistic motion blur, restrained handheld movement only during the final escape. Preserve screen direction, wardrobe, faces, prop placement, and architectural continuity.

SOUND DIRECTION:
Elevator motor hum, faint headphone music that fades when the headphones are lowered, clothing rustle, subtle knife click, fluorescent buzz, elevator chime, measured footsteps, resistant key mechanism, and a heavy final door slam. Minimal low strings and a gradually accelerating bass pulse. No dialogue or voiceover.
AVOID:
On-screen captions, subtitles, titles, watermarks, logos, exaggerated combat, gore, artificial skin, distorted hands, changing faces, duplicated characters, impossible camera passes through walls, random slow motion, excessive lens flares, or frantic montage. Build suspense through glances, blocking, sound, and timing.
```

```text
Create a 10-second ultra-realistic cinematic sci-fi fantasy video in vertical 9:16 format.

MAIN CHARACTER

A beautiful young adult woman in her 20s walks alone through a futuristic city at night. She has long, naturally flowing dark hair and a calm, slightly curious expression.

She is wearing an elegant floor-length flowing gown made from lightweight, ethereal fabric. The gown is sophisticated and cinematic, with long flowing layers that gently move with the wind as she walks. The fabric catches reflections from the city’s lights. No revealing clothing.

Keep her appearance, hairstyle, face, gown, and proportions consistent throughout the entire video.

ENVIRONMENT

The city is enormous, futuristic, and almost completely silent. Towering glass skyscrapers disappear into the clouds, covered with subtle neon lights and glowing windows. The streets are wet from recent rain, creating beautiful reflections.

There is a light mist in the air, tiny floating particles, distant flying vehicles, and soft atmospheric fog.

The overall atmosphere should feel mysterious, beautiful, surreal, peaceful, and slightly eerie.

0–2 SECONDS

Start with a cinematic medium-wide tracking shot from behind the woman.

She walks slowly down a quiet futuristic street, her long gown trailing naturally behind her.

The camera smoothly follows her at walking height. Her footsteps create tiny ripples in shallow rainwater on the pavement.

She suddenly slows down.

The ambient city lights reflect across the wet street.

2–4 SECONDS

The woman gradually looks upward.

The camera slowly tilts up with her gaze.

Reveal an enormous ocean floating impossibly above the city, suspended in the night sky between the skyscrapers.

The ocean appears like a gigantic transparent body of water held in the sky, with sunlight-like blue illumination filtering through its depths.

Inside the floating ocean, massive whales slowly swim above the skyscrapers.

Their enormous silhouettes pass through the glowing blue water overhead.

Make the whales realistic and majestic, moving naturally and slowly.

The woman remains completely still for a moment, staring upward in silent amazement.

4–6 SECONDS

Cut to a closer cinematic shot of the woman looking upward.

Suddenly, tiny droplets of water begin appearing above the wet pavement.

Instead of falling down, the droplets rise upward against gravity.

Hundreds of tiny droplets lift from puddles and the streets and float toward the enormous ocean above.

The droplets should move smoothly and physically naturally, leaving subtle trails of reflected city light.

The woman slowly raises one hand and watches the water droplets pass her fingers.

6–8 SECONDS

The phenomenon becomes much larger.

Water begins rising from fountains, puddles, rooftops, and streets throughout the city.

The camera pulls backward and upward into a wide establishing shot.

The entire futuristic city begins to slowly rise with the ascending water.

Buildings gently lift from the ground, remaining structurally intact.

Lights flicker softly across the skyscrapers.

Flying vehicles hover in confusion while the city slowly becomes weightless.

The woman’s gown and hair float slightly upward as gravity begins to disappear.

8–10 SECONDS

Finish with a breathtaking wide aerial cinematic shot.

The entire futuristic city is now slowly ascending toward the enormous floating ocean.

The woman stands on a rooftop or elevated street below, appearing tiny against the gigantic spectacle.

Whales glide peacefully through the ocean above her.

Thousands of glowing water droplets rise around the city like stars.

The camera slowly pulls farther away, revealing the impossible scale of the scene.

End on a beautiful surreal image of the woman, the floating city, the enormous ocean, and whales swimming overhead, all suspended between the night sky and the glowing city.
```

```text
30-second cinematic fashion-travel music video following one beautiful sweet slim young woman across New Zealand — starting on a rainy Auckland street, moving through a coastal train journey, green countryside and finally a dramatic black-sand beach where she dances with a friend. Premium outdoor fashion commercial meets cinematic road-trip film.
Cool blue rain, emerald greens, volcanic black sand, warm sunset skin tones.
Natural film grain, subtle lens bloom, realistic moisture and atmospheric perspective.
24fps, 180-degree shutter.

LOOK B 6.5–15.5s:

LOOK C 15.5–30s:
Graphite Nike lightweight track jacket, loose black trousers, white sneakers, small silver hoop earrings.

No Adidas. No Puma. Nike branding stays physically correct and understated.

[LOCATIONS]
LOC 1 — Auckland rainy street:
Glass towers, wet pavement, neon reflections, green trees, moving pedestrians and buses.

LOC 2 — Historic railway platform:
Dark green station canopy, old benches, rain droplets, passengers waiting naturally.

LOC 3 — Coastal train:

Small wooden café, surfboards, warm interior lights.

Open black-sand area with distant cliffs and sea.

0.0–2.2s
SHOT 1 — RAIN OPEN
50mm handheld close-up. Woman walks beneath light rain in LOOK A. Drops collect on blue jacket. She smiles while listening to music.

2.2–3.8s
SHOT 2 — CAMERA DETAIL
85mm ECU. Her fingers switch Sony camera mode. White earphone cable crosses jacket naturally.

3.8–6.5s

35mm tracking shot. She crosses wet street, turns toward camera with a small smile. Bus passes behind, creating natural foreground wipe.

6.5–8.6s

Hard cut on bus wipe. LOOK B. She walks onto railway platform holding camera and cream backpack.

8.6–10.4s

24mm. Train enters frame, wind moves her hair and fleece. She looks toward it.

10.4–12.6s

50mm inside train. She sits beside window, earphones visible, cheek against glass, watching countryside.

12.6–14.2s

14.2–16.0s

She steps down from train.
Huge yellow condensed text:

appears vertically beside her as she walks forward.

16.0–18.0s

24mm wide. LOOK C begins. She walks through a grassy mountain road, camera tracking sideways. Clouds move across distant hills.

18.0–19.8s
SHOT 10 — WATERFALL
Wide 24mm. She stands near wet wooden trail, turns toward massive waterfall and laughs.

19.8–21.6s

Hard cut. She walks over grassy cliff path. Camera starts behind her and gently moves beside her, revealing black-sand beach below.

21.6–23.2s

23.2–25.0s

35mm two-shot. Both walk barefoot along black sand, shoes in hands, laughing naturally. Wind moves their clothes.

25.0–26.4s

85mm. Friend takes Sony camera from lead and quickly photographs her. Lead laughs and covers face playfully.

26.4–28.0s

28.0–30.0s

Wide-to-medium. They stop near beach café and dance freely to music, no choreography.
At 28.6s bold yellow condensed text hits:
【KEEP MOVING】
Then at 29.3s:
【KEEP DREAMING】
Both stacked over the dancing pair.

Hard cut to black.

[CAMERA / MOVEMENT]
Rain shots: handheld 50mm.
Train: locked and intimate 50–85mm.
Landscape: 24mm with natural atmospheric depth.
Beach: low 24mm and gentle lateral tracking.
No drone orbit.
No crash zoom.

Wet pavement reflects real light.
Hair reacts to wind and moisture.

Fleece and nylon wrinkle naturally.

Countryside: diffused cloud light.

Camera shutter.
Distant waterfall.
Ocean wind.
Footsteps on wet sand.

Friend only from 21.6s.
```

<img src="covers/2096984350055436729.jpg" alt="Image 1" width="480" />

**Prompt**

```text
【Style】 Live-action realism, vertical extreme adventure documentary short video, handheld smartphone wide-angle photography texture. Real person, authentic cabin interior, refraction and internal fractures of transparent ice, black volcanic rock and orange-red lava. Retain handheld micro-jitters, flight vibrations, natural motion blur, and exposure shifts. Dark interior cabin, bright sky outside, lava casting orange-red reflections upward onto the person's arms, the ice block, and the door threshold. The final eruption utilizes realistic cinematic visual effects, with a three-dimensional, volumetric white steam column with curling details.
【Duration】 15 seconds, 9:16 vertical screen, one continuous take throughout. Real-time action, no cuts, no slow motion, no cutaways to other angles.
【Scene】 A helicopter hovers above a volcanic lava zone with its side door completely open. The camera is located inside the cabin, near the open door, shooting toward the person in the doorway and the exterior. The dark door frame and inner cabin wall are visible on the left and top; below is dark gray anti-slip textured flooring, a metal threshold, and tie-down hardware. Outside and below stretches a vast expanse of dark gray-black solidified lava crust, covered with winding, glowing orange-red fissures. The fissures surround a nearly circular bright lava pond, where golden-yellow and orange-red molten material slowly churns, locally forming bubbles, bright spots, and broken dark floating crust. Thin gray smoke drifts along the surface. The circular lava pond is positioned directly below the cabin door, serving as the single impact point for both the falling ice and the subsequent steam eruption.
【Character】 An adult East Asian woman, long brown hair tied in a ponytail, wearing a technical shell jacket, gray-green cargo pants, dark boots, a black safety harness around her body, and aviation headphones with a boom mic. Her hair is continuously blown toward the side and back by the strong wind outside the door. She starts with a relaxed expression, first smiling at the camera, then turning her attention outside; at the end, startled by the rising white steam column, she quickly bends down and retreats back into the cabin. The character remains inside the cabin at all times, only extending her arms and the ice block outside the door, never jumping out of the helicopter.
【Core Prop】 A single massive transparent rectangular ice block, roughly extending from the woman's chest down to her knees, with a width close to her torso width. The ice block is heavy and solid, featuring defined top, front, side faces, and depth, with slightly melted edges, milky-white interior ice haze, fine air bubbles, and irregular fracture lines, wet on its surface. The woman grips the left and right sides of the ice with both hands, forearms clamping and supporting it to convey its heavy weight. The ice stays vertical while held; after being pushed out the door, it tilts and falls, tumbling only slightly, never turning into thin glass, plastic bags, liquid, or multiple fragments.
【Camera】 Opens with a close-up handheld medium shot; the woman is on the left side of the frame, the giant ice block is centered-right, and the bright space outside the door is in the right background. The camera is about one meter away from the woman, capturing her head, torso, bent legs, and the entire ice block simultaneously. After the ice is released, the camera tilts down and to the right, peering over the threshold to continuously track the falling ice. The camera stays by the cabin door at all times, never flying out with the ice. It then maintains a high-angle overhead view of the lava pond, finally quickly pulling back into the cabin as the steam column approaches.
[00:00–00:01.30] Camera Action 1: Holding giant ice, smiling toward camera. The woman stands sideways by the open cabin door, feet staggered, knees slightly bent. The massive rectangular ice block stands in front of her chest and abdomen, its bottom suspended above the floor. She grips both sides with her hands, elbows bent, clamping the ice securely before her. She looks at the camera with the corners of her mouth curved upward, briefly showing an excited, relaxed smile. The wind blows her ponytail and loose hair to the back left. The ice block sways slightly with her arms and the helicopter's vibration, yet maintains its complete shape. The ice surface reflects the bright sky, and the internal white fractures are clearly visible. The camera maintains a close-up medium shot with slight handheld shake; the gray-black volcanic landscape outside is visible on the right. Audio: Continuous helicopter rotor thrum, cabin vibration, and wind blowing through the door.
[00:01.30–00:02.30] Camera Action 2: Turning head to look at drop point, bending knees and shifting center of gravity. The woman shifts her gaze away from the camera, first looking right toward the exterior, then looking down at the lava pond below. Her shoulders and torso rotate toward the door, legs bending further as her center of gravity lowers. She shifts the ice outward from against her chest, arms gradually extending, bringing the bottom of the ice near the metal threshold. Instead of a sudden throw, she conveys clear physical strain, slowly and continuously extending the entire ice block out the door. The side of the ice facing the lava gradually catches orange-red reflections, while the side facing away remains cold, white, and transparent. The camera begins to tilt slightly right and downward, bringing the ice block and threshold into focus. Audio: Continued rotor sound, rustle of clothing and safety harness, short, strained exhale from the character.
[00:02.30–00:03.10] Camera Action 3: Pushing over the threshold, fingers releasing. Bending her knees and leaning forward, the woman continues extending her arms out the door, moving the ice's center of gravity past the threshold. The ice remains nearly upright at first, then its top tilts outward, the bottom clearing the doorway area. She straightens her arms, releasing her fingers sequentially to let the ice go completely. Her palms linger briefly in the air before pulling back. The ice begins to fall downward and outward, its top rotating slightly, remaining the same solid, heavy rectangular block. The camera immediately tilts downward following the ice's trajectory; the woman's face leaves the frame, with only her arms briefly remaining in the upper-left; the threshold and anti-slip floor stay in the lower foreground. Audio: Wind intensifies, low-frequency rotor hum continues, no glass-shattering sounds.
[00:03.10–00:04.60] Camera Action 4: Ice moving away, overhead tracking toward drop point. The ice quickly transitions from a close-up outside the door to a small rectangular block far below, following a continuous downward arc toward the circular lava pond. The ice tilts and tumbles slightly, its top and sides alternating reflections, the downward-facing side illuminated in pink-orange by the lava, while its edges remain translucent cold white. The camera keeps tilting downward, centering the bright circular lava pond in the frame. An angled metal threshold remains in the lower-left corner to establish that the camera is still on the helicopter. ```text
```
```text
Seedance 2.5 Video Generation Prompt
Mira vs Gatekeeper / Teleportation Temple - Entrance Straight Hallway
Ultra-high-speed acrobatic hand-to-hand combat · Gatekeeper's decisive strike · Mira's defeat
Final Runtime: 15.0 seconds / 16:9 / Full-color hand-drawn 2D animation

[Completion Conditions for this Session]

From the beginning to the middle phase, Mira and the Gatekeeper engage in an evenly matched, ultra-high-speed close-quarters battle.

Mira does not engage in a simple exchange of punches. Using a one-two combination of a left jab and right straight to draw the Gatekeeper's focus and both arms to the front, she chains into side kicks, roundhouse kicks, knee strikes, back kicks, and leaping attacks.

The basic combo structure is as follows:
One-two
→ Gatekeeper parries with open palms
→ Mira converts the parried shoulder rotation into a kick
→ Gatekeeper dodges or guards the kick
→ Mira tucks her kicking leg
→ Springs off the floor, wall, or pillar
→ Changes direction mid-air
→ Transitions into the next combo upon landing

Mira does not stay fighting directly in front of the Gatekeeper. Using high-speed side steps, wall kicks, leaps off pillars, cartwheel-like evasions, backflips, and mid-air body twists, she aggressively shifts to the Gatekeeper's flanks and rear.

In the middle phase, Mira circles rapidly around the Gatekeeper at afterimage speed to reach his rear. Arriving behind him, Mira does not stop; channeling the landing rebound, she unleashes a one-two and a spinning kick.

In the final phase, the Gatekeeper anticipates the landing spot of Mira's high-speed combo. Sweeping both of Mira's arms to one side, he lands a right palm strike square into her defenseless solar plexus.

Mira is blown violently backwards, crashes into the stone wall, and drops to the floor. The scene ends in her defeat as she tries and fails to stand up, unable to resume combat.

[Order of Instruction Priority]

① Character consistency of Mira and the Gatekeeper, barehanded combat, attacker/defender roles, hit reactions, outcome of the fight.
② 15.0-second runtime, shot order, screen direction, kinetic continuity between cuts.
③ Mira's combo chaining from one-two punches into kicks.
④ Mira's acrobatic movement and high-speed flanking.
⑤ High-angle bird's-eye views, follow-pans from behind, diagonal tracking shots, solo framing shots.
⑥ Contour dissolution, trailing smearing, smears, afterimages.
⑦ Audio, impact sounds, cloth rustling, footsteps, stone resonance.

Prioritize characters, actions, sequence, and screen directions detailed in the Timed Direction breakdown.

[Reference Materials]

Temple Space Reference:
[Provided]

Mira (Protagonist).png

Gatekeeper Character Reference:
[Provided]
Both Gatekeeper reference images depict the exact same single individual. Do not increase the number to two.

Audio Materials:
User-provided original audio dedicated exclusively to Mira.
User-provided original audio dedicated exclusively to the Gatekeeper.

Assign Mira's audio only to Mira.
Assign Gatekeeper's audio only to the Gatekeeper.
Do not swap their voices.

Neither character speaks any dialogue in this sequence. Use only non-verbal sounds included in the audio materials: breathing, vocal exertion grunts, short battle cries, and hit-reaction gasps.

Do not generate any new shouts, dialogue, screams, or monologues not included in the audio materials. Never synchronize the Gatekeeper's exposed mouth to Mira's audio.

[Mira's Fixed Character Design]

Height: 164 cm. Silver-white short bob cut. Cold, sharp eyes. Black hooded cape with crimson lining and gold runic embroidery. Large red gem brooch. Black corset belt. Layered black gothic skirt with white ruffled hem. Dark tights. Black lace-up combat boots.

Do not draw any ear jewelry on Mira.

Remove all earrings, studs, hoops, dangling ear jewels, or metallic ear accessories. The only red gem is the brooch on her chest. Do not duplicate red gems onto her ears.

Completely barehanded throughout. Do not give her swords, daggers, staves, or any other weapons.

Karate-based combat style. Uses left jab, right straight, side kicks, roundhouse kicks, back kicks, knee strikes, elbow strikes, sweeps, forearm guards, and parrying deflections.

Do not string punches back and forth repeatedly on their own; use the one-two strictly as an entry into kicks or leaps.

[Gatekeeper's Fixed Character Design]

Height: 187 cm male. Combat helmet and mask with black horn-like protrusions. Only the lower mouth area is exposed. White cross-insignia tabard. Black long robe. Wide gray trousers. Black gloves. Buckled boots.

Consolidate the traits from both reference images into a single Gatekeeper.

Completely barehanded throughout. Do not produce spears, swords, shields, or any other weapons.

Refined Chinese martial arts base. Employs palm parries, forearm guards, shoulder rotations, hip twists, short foot shifts, low stances, and spinning hook kicks.

The Gatekeeper's composure is expressed through precise, minimal movements slipping off attack lines, not through standing stiff.

In the first half, the Gatekeeper does not maintain one-sided dominance. Mira's combos push him back half a step, graze his helmet with a kick, seize his flank and rear, and force him to guard body blows.

[Visual Format]

Full-color hand-drawn 2D animation. Natural flat colors. Minimal cel shading. Subtle line weight variations preserved.

Do not render human bodies cleanly and uniformly across all frames. In intermediate poses at peak speed, temporarily dissolve, stretch, and break contours, snapping them back to clear form in the next recognizable key pose.

Retain the structural order: face, chest, hips, supporting leg, attacking limb. Even when deconstructing contours, do not liquefy the characters entirely.

No photographic motion blur, no excessive depth-of-field blurring, no 3DCG afterimage effects.

[Contour Smearing and Trailing]

The moment a character surges forward at high speed, lead with the face, chest, fists, knees, or toes in the direction of motion.

Trail the contours of the back of the head, hair, cape, sleeves, skirt, tabard, and trailing leg in the opposite direction.

Accelerating toward screen right:
The front of the body moves right first.
Hair, clothing, and back contours are dragged behind toward the left.
Right before contact, the trailing contours snap back into alignment with the body.

Shifting back toward screen left:
Hips and supporting leg rotate left first.
Shoulders, hair, and clothing linger momentarily in the previous rightward vector.
Before the old contour fades, superimpose a new leftward smear over it.

Contours may break apart during acceleration. Restore standard anatomy upon impact, successful evasion, landing, receiving a hit, or key facial expressions.

[Afterimages]

Apply two to three stages of full-body afterimages solely during the sequence where Mira circles the Gatekeeper at high speed.

Afterimages are broken trailing contours left at positions Mira just vacated. They are not duplicate characters or shadow clones. Never have afterimages execute separate attacks.

Oldest afterimage: A few sparse lines tracing hair, cape, waist, and legs.
Middle afterimage: Discernible body orientation and planted leg.
Physical Mira: Snaps back to standard anatomical form the instant she lands behind the Gatekeeper.

Fade afterimages sequentially from oldest to newest, absorbing the final contour smear into the motion of her black cape.

[Prohibition of VFX and Manga Shorthand]

Do not use glowing shockwaves, magical glows, star-shaped hit sparks, black speed/focus lines, manga radiating lines, crescent-shaped motion arcs, floor movement trails, or screen-covering flashes.

Express the force of punches, kicks, parries, and knockbacks through anatomical compression, footwork bracing, post-impact displacement, lagging hair and cloth, camera shake, blunt impact sounds, floor dust, and stone wall fracturing.

Normal contact must not damage the stone floor or walls. Only the final impact point where Mira slams into the wall develops minor cracks and falling stone dust post-collision.

[Setting]

A roofed straight corridor located at the entrance side of the underground Teleportation Temple.

A 22-meter-wide stone corridor. Retain the rectangular stone flooring, longitudinal seam joints, flanking Gothic stone pillars, pointed-arch wall niches, blue decorative drapery between pillars, and amber wall sconce torches.

Do not transition into the circular magic circle chamber.

Do not depict the circular hall, central magic circle, glowing circular floor, or combat circling a magic circle.

Even in top-down shots, the floor must remain a rectangular hallway with linear stone seams. No circular floor patterns.

The entrance remains out of frame by default. Do not create an independent shot solely to showcase the entrance.

If the entrance accidentally appears in the background, its 6-meter double doors stand open inward, with the entire opening sealed seamlessly by an opaque, viscous silver curtain.

The silver curtain has zero transparency. No scenery, light, or silhouettes show through. It is a thick, fluid silver surface that heaves, swells, droops, stretches, and flows with thick, irregular viscosity.

Do not alter it into a transparent water curtain, mist, thin fabric, mirror, or rigid silver plate.

[Camera Setup]

POV is strictly limited to Shot 15, the single instance where Mira receives the final palm strike. No POV anywhere else.

The pure profile side-view framing is strictly limited to Shot 16, capturing Mira flying back from an alternate angle.

Standard combat framing centers on the following compositions:
· Low follow-pan tracking from Mira's diagonal rear.
· Follow-pan tracking Mira's attacks from the Gatekeeper's diagonal rear.
· High-angle bird's-eye view mapping footwork and flanking arcs.
· Diagonal tracking shots sweeping massive foreground pillars across frame.
· Low full shot tracking Mira alone.
· Medium shot framing the Gatekeeper's dodges and counters alone.
· Solo shot of the Gatekeeper showcasing the setup for the decisive blow.
· High-angle look-down framing only the defeated Mira.

In follow-pans from behind, do not lock the camera rigidly to the character's back. The subject accelerates first; the camera lags momentarily, then catches up via rapid whip-pan and short push-in.

Tracking behind Mira: Frame the Gatekeeper forward over her cape. Tracking behind the Gatekeeper: Frame Mira closing in while shifting vectors over his white tabard.

During key leaps, dodges, guards, decisive strikes, knockbacks, and the defeat, focus exclusively on a single primary subject. Do not force the opponent's entire body into the background.

[High-Speed Combat Engine]

Each combo smoothly connects into the next movement chain:
One-two
→ Kick
→ Opponent's defense
→ Kick recovery
→ Landing or foot switch
→ Leap, dodge, or rapid flanking
→ Subsequent one-two or aerial strike

No resetting to neutral stances after attacks. No extended freezes after guarding. Do not wait until fully landed to initiate an attack; start the next offensive chain directly out of the landing posture.

Never have Mira glide across the floor standing upright. Ground traversal must utilize lunges, step adjustments, side steps, landing compression, and explosive push-offs.

Do not use prolonged sliding even after Mira is struck by the palm strike. She is sent flying through the air, impacts the wall, and drops.

[Timed Shot Breakdown]

Shot 1 / 0.0 - 0.5s
Extreme close-up of Mira's face pulling violently into a low-angle shot.

0.0 - 0.2s:
Mira's cold gaze locks onto the Gatekeeper ahead. Her ears are completely bare. Silver-white bangs flutter; her shoulders lead the forward surge.

0.2 - 0.5s:
Mira kicks off the floor with her right foot. Face and chest thrust forward into depth first; hair, cape, and back contours trail toward the foreground. Camera pulls back aggressively, offering a brief glimpse of the closing Gatekeeper.

Shot 2 / 0.5 - 1.6s
Low follow-pan tracking from Mira's diagonal rear.

0.5 - 0.8s:
Camera pushes forward behind Mira's right shoulder. Mira steps in with her left foot, firing a left jab. She drives her right hip forward into a right straight. The Gatekeeper deflects both fists outward using his left and right palms.

0.8 - 1.1s:
Without breaking the parried right shoulder's momentum, Mira raises her right knee. Pivoting on the ball of her planted left foot, she snaps a side kick into the Gatekeeper's flank.

1.1 - 1.3s:
The Gatekeeper angles his left forearm down to deflect the kick outward. Mira instantly tucks her right knee, planting her right foot at the Gatekeeper's side.

1.3 - 1.6s:
Loading weight onto her planted right foot, Mira drops her stance low. Stepping her left foot wide, she initiates an outward flanking arc around the Gatekeeper. The camera executes a fast pan, briefly occluded by her flowing cape.

Shot 3 / 1.6 - 2.4s
Follow-pan tracking from the Gatekeeper's diagonal rear.

1.6 - 1.9s:
Framing Mira past his white tabard. Circling to the Gatekeeper's flank, Mira plants her left foot and whips a low roundhouse kick aimed above the Gatekeeper's knee.

1.9 - 2.1s:
The Gatekeeper pulls his right leg back to evade the kick. As his rear right foot plants, he counters with a right palm thrust toward Mira's shoulder.

2.1 - 2.4s:
Mira deflects the palm outward with her left forearm, dipping her torso forward. Briefly touching her right hand to the floor, she slips under the palm into a dynamic cartwheel evasion. Camera tracks Mira rotating over the Gatekeeper's shoulder.

2.9 - 3.2s:

Mira connects with a left step, planting the sole of her right boot firmly against the nearby stone wall. Right knee bends deeply, hips coiling toward the Gatekeeper.

The boot grazes the protrusion of his helmet. The helmet does not shatter or come off. The graze knocks the Gatekeeper's head a few centimeters sideways; his tabard ripples delayed by the displaced air.

4.4 - 4.6s:
The Gatekeeper presses his right palm against the outside of Mira's ankle, guiding the kick past. He does not shove back, instead riding Mira's rotational velocity.

Shot 7 / 4.6 - 5.5s
High overhead view. Landing combo.

4.6 - 4.8s:
Mira lands on the floor right foot first, flexing her right knee deeply to absorb and compress her kinetic energy.

4.8 - 5.1s:
Springing from the compressed right leg, she fires a left jab and right straight one-two. Backpedaling, the Gatekeeper deflects both with alternating palms. No repeating punches in place.

5.1 - 5.3s:
Channelling hip rotation from the right straight, Mira drives her left knee up into a left roundhouse kick toward the Gatekeeper's upper body.

5.3 - 5.5s:
The Gatekeeper absorbs the kick with both forearms, shoved back half a step. Mira recoils her left knee, resetting her body toward a landing stance.

Shot 8 / 5.5 - 6.4s
Follow-pan tracking from Mira's diagonal rear. Initiation of high-speed flank.

5.5 - 5.7s:
Mira's left foot touches down. Knees flexed, she steps her right foot wide outside the Gatekeeper, gaze anchored firmly on him.

5.7 - 5.9s:
Loading her right leg, she drives hard sideways off the stone. Her chest and hips burst screen right; hair, cape, sleeves, skirt, and left-side contours smear behind toward screen left.

5.9 - 6.2s:
Mira circles the Gatekeeper in a low, lightning-fast side-dash. Her true form flashes out of frame; the camera lags, tracking the lingering contour smears before whipping right.

6.2 - 6.4s:
A faint, broken afterimage lingers where she passed his front flank; a second afterimage appears as she clears his side. Physical Mira surges completely behind him.

Shot 9 / 6.4 - 7.1s
High-angle bird's-eye view. High-speed flank and positional inversion.

6.4 - 6.6s:
The Gatekeeper pivots to track Mira, but his eye-line lags at the previous afterimage. Mira's true body traces a wide arc around his perimeter.

6.6 - 6.8s:
Mira plants her left foot wide, pivoting violently on the ball of her foot to swing her body around, whipping her right foot directly behind the Gatekeeper.

6.8 - 7.1s:
Her right foot plants firmly behind the Gatekeeper. Physical Mira instantly consolidates into solid anatomy. The older afterimages dissipate sequentially, the final trailing smear folding into the sweep of her black cape.

Shot 10 / 7.1 - 8.0s
Close follow-pan tracking over the Gatekeeper's diagonal rear.

7.1 - 7.3s:
From right behind the Gatekeeper, Mira launches a left jab out of her landing rebound. The Gatekeeper reacts over his right shoulder, whipping his body around.

7.3 - 7.5s:
Mira follows up with a right straight to his ribs. The Gatekeeper intercepts it with his left forearm, but the force slides his footing half a step wide.

7.5 - 7.8s:
Mira snaps her right fist back, driving hip torque into a high spinning left kick aimed at his head. The Gatekeeper raises his right forearm to guard.

7.8 - 8.0s:
The kick collides with his forearm, buckling his elbow and tilting his torso. Mira snaps her left leg back, setting up her next touchdown.

Shot 11 / 8.0 - 8.8s
Low full shot tracking the Gatekeeper alone from a diagonal front angle.

8.0 - 8.2s:
The Gatekeeper resets his displaced right foot, loading weight onto his left. Without winding up, he rotates his hips with clinical precision.

8.2 - 8.5s:
He sweeps his right leg upward from the outside into a spinning hook kick aimed at Mira. His supporting leg extends, lifting him slightly off the stones. His kicking leg smears along its strike arc.

8.5 - 8.8s:
His right heel whips toward Mira off-screen. Camera pivots toward the Gatekeeper's rear, tracking the impact vector.

Shot 12 / 8.8 - 9.7s
Low full shot of Mira alone. Guarding into pillar counter-leap.

8.8 - 9.0s:
The Gatekeeper's right heel strikes in from off-frame, caught by Mira's crossed forearms. Her elbows compress; her shoulders and torso are driven back toward a stone pillar.

9.0 - 9.2s:
Mira plants her right foot in the direction of the recoil. Bending her knee, she wedges the sole of her left foot low against the pillar base.

9.2 - 9.5s:
Extending her left leg, she rockets diagonally upward off the pillar. Hair and cape lag below; her chest and hips rise first.

9.5 - 9.7s:
Tucking forward in mid-air, she enters a tight front flip clearing the Gatekeeper's head, pulling her legs tight to minimize rotational axis.

Shot 13 / 9.7 - 10.5s
High diagonal bird's-eye view. Overhead vault into landing combo.

9.7 - 9.9s:
Mira flips cleanly over the Gatekeeper's head. The Gatekeeper twists his torso, turning toward her anticipated landing zone.

9.9 - 10.1s:
Mira touches down right foot then left foot. From deep knee compression, she unleashes a left jab and right straight.

10.1 - 10.3s:
The Gatekeeper sweeps both punches outward with his palms. Mira pivots her right shoulder rotation into a driving right knee strike aimed at his midsection.

10.3 - 10.5s:
The Gatekeeper pulls his hips back, shifting the knee's vector outward with his forearm. Mira snaps her right knee down, planting close beside him.

Shot 14 / 10.5 - 11.4s
Medium shot tracking from the Gatekeeper's diagonal rear. Revealing the setup for the decisive blow.

10.5 - 10.7s:
Mira plants her weight on her right foot, initiating a final left jab, right straight, and left roundhouse combo.

10.7 - 10.9s:
The Gatekeeper sweeps the left jab aside with his right palm. Sliding his left palm along her right straight, he guides Mira's right wrist across to his left side.

10.9 - 11.1s:
Mira pulls her right fist back while trying to chamber her left kick. But the Gatekeeper steps his left foot deep between them, using his left palm to pin her right arm directly over her left forearm.

11.1 - 11.4s:
Mira's forearms are pinned crossways to one side for a split second, completely exposing her centerline. Bracing off his rear right foot, the Gatekeeper drives his right hip, right shoulder, and right palm forward in unbroken kinetic alignment. Cut just before the palm strike hits her solar plexus.

Shot 15 / 11.4 - 11.9s
The only POV shot in the sequence: Mira's perspective.

11.4 - 11.6s:
The Gatekeeper directly in front. At the bottom of the frame, Mira's own forearms are crossed and pinned to one side. The Gatekeeper's right palm strike lunges straight toward her solar plexus below camera level.

11.6 - 11.7s:
Mira tries to pull her arms back, but she is too late. The Gatekeeper's right palm strike impacts her solar plexus. Do not whitewash the screen; preserve the visceral impact vector of his arm against her torso.

11.7 - 11.9s:
Perspective rockets violently backward. The Gatekeeper's figure recedes instantly; background pillars blur sideways. Mira's head snaps back on a delay, tilting the horizon sharply. Hard cut mid-recoil.

Shot 16 / 11.9 - 13.3s
Alternate wide shot framing the hallway and stone wall diagonally. The only lateral profile composition.

11.9 - 12.1s:
Mira flies into frame from the far left at extreme velocity, entirely airborne, hurling from left to right. She is not suspended in the center from the start.

12.1 - 12.4s:
Chest and hips lead the flight path; head, hair, cape, skirt, and legs stretch in heavy trailing smears back toward the left. Two faint, broken afterimages trail behind her.

12.4 - 12.7s:
Camera lags slightly, catching up with a hard right pan. Mira's back and right shoulder crash violently into the stone wall. Her anatomy snaps back to solid form upon impact.

12.7 - 13.0s:
Her impacting back and shoulder compress abruptly. Small fractures spread across the impact zone on the stone wall, followed by a shower of fine stone dust. The wall must not explode.

13.0 - 13.3s:
Her momentum halted, Mira rebounds faintly off the wall and drops. Her fingers scrape weakly against the stone, unable to find purchase. One boot clips the floor, but her knees buckle completely under her.

Shot 17 / 13.3 - 14.2s
Cut on action. High-angle look-down. Framing Mira alone.

13.3 - 13.6s:
Mira's hips, then shoulders slam down onto the stone floor. Her body rolls once to the side. Both the cracked stone wall and linear floor seams are visible.

13.6 - 13.9s:
Mira presses her right palm flat against the floor, straining to lift her upper body. Her left arm clutches tightly against her battered abdomen. Her right elbow trembles violently, lifting her shoulder only slightly.

13.9 - 14.2s:
Her right elbow gives out entirely. Her torso collapses back to the cold stone; she cannot bring her knees beneath her. Do not add the Gatekeeper to the background.

Shot 18 / 14.2 - 15.0s
Close-up of Mira's face, shoulder, and hand on the floor. Slightly elevated look-down angle.

14.2 - 14.5s:
Continuing the collapse in close-up as her shoulder hits the stone. Silver-white bangs spill across her cheek. Her ear is completely bare of jewelry. Stone dust dusts her black cape.

14.5 - 14.7s:
Mira shifts her eyes toward the off-screen Gatekeeper. Unable to lift her head, only her pupils move slightly. Her left arm remains clutching her stomach.

14.7 - 15.0s:
Tension bleeds from her right hand resting on the floor. Her fingers loosen, her shoulder slumps. Conscious and breathing, but completely unable to rise or resume combat.

Do not make her cry.
Do not make her smile.
Do not have her take a combat stance again.
Do not cut to a victory pose of the Gatekeeper.
Fade out on defeated Mira's breathing, hair, torn garments, and settling stone dust.

[Audio and Sound Effects]

No background music (BGM).

Use exclusively the user-provided original audio for Mira.
Use exclusively the user-provided original audio for the Gatekeeper.

Neither character speaks any dialogue.

Synchronize only non-verbal audio cues from the provided materials to the corresponding physical actions:

Mira:
Sharp breath on leaps.
Short grunts of vocal exertion on heavy kicks.
Stifled gasp when blocking the hook kick with crossed forearms.
Choked, suffocated gasp immediately upon taking the decisive palm strike.
Ragged, labored breathing in the final moments struggling on the floor.

Gatekeeper:
Calm, controlled exhalations when parrying Mira's assaults.
Short grunt releasing power into the spinning hook kick.
Sharp kiai battle shout channeling full-body force into the final right palm strike.
Short, contained vocal grunts when absorbing Mira's strikes.

Do not synthesize any new voices if a specific sound is missing from the provided audio.

One-two punches feature sharp, differing impact snaps. Kicks yield deeper, heavier thuds than punches. Palm deflections produce dry, concise slapping sounds. Wall pushes give a solid crack of boot soles on stone. During the rapid flank, pan a rushing wind sound along Mira's travel vector.

Just before the final palm strike, quiet all audio down to brief scuffing steps and rustling fabric. The palm strike lands with an explosive crack layered with a deep low-end thump. A fierce rushing wind effect carries through the cut as she flies. Her wall collision crashes with heavy stone resonance, followed by the clatter of pebbles and stone dust.

[Prohibitions]

Do not use POV outside of Shot 15.
Do not use profile side-views outside of Shot 16.
Do not frame both characters together in a static full shot throughout.
Do not draw ear jewelry on Mira.
Do not duplicate her chest brooch onto her ears.
Do not have Mira engage in extended punch-only trades.
Always chain one-twos into kicks, vaults, or flanks.
Do not slide Mira across the floor in an upright pose.
Do not turn afterimages into clone figures or separate entities.
Never allow afterimages to execute independent attacks.
No photographic motion blur.
No VFX, glows, shockwaves, speed lines, hit stars, or comic book symbols.
Do not give weapons to either character.
Do not give the Gatekeeper one-sided dominance in the first half.
Do not severely injure Mira before the decisive blow.
Do not alter the sequence: palm strike → knockback → wall impact → drop → failed attempt to rise.
Do not let Mira stand back up at the end.
Do not give the Gatekeeper a follow-up attack.
Do not transition into the circular magic circle chamber.
Do not generate a transparent silver veil.
Do not add dialogue, BGM, subtitles, logos, watermarks, or on-screen UI.

[Final Objective]

Map footwork and position exchanges via bird's-eye views, and immerse the viewer in raw speed using dynamic follow-pans trailing Mira or the Gatekeeper's diagonal rear.

Mira does not spam repetitive punches; she uses the one-two to draw the Gatekeeper's guard forward, chaining seamlessly into kicks, cartwheels, wall leaps, pillar bounds, and lightning flanks.

At Mira's maximum velocity, body extremities lead the vector while hair, cape, skirt, and outer contours drag behind. The camera lags behind her speed, re-acquiring her solid form only after sweeping past afterimages to catch her landing behind the Gatekeeper.

In the climax, the Gatekeeper reads Mira's landing point. Sweeping both her arms aside, he drives an unyielding palm strike into her solar plexus.

Deliver the impact viscously through a single brief POV, cutting instantly to an alternate angle of her flying through the air, smashing into the stone wall, collapsing to the floor, and ending in helpless defeat as she fails to rise.
```
