<a name="prompt-2101376938258624640"></a>

### Blue and red colored pencil sketch samurai awaken from a sketchbook, engaging in a high-speed macro duel on a desk triggering chain-reaction destruction, before returning to their places right before grandpa appears.

Author：[@abulu8](https://x.com/abulu8) · [Source](https://x.com/abulu8/status/2101376938258624640)

Sketch / Line Art · Published

**Summary:** Blue and red colored pencil sketch samurai awaken from a sketchbook, engaging in a high-speed macro duel on a desk triggering chain-reaction destruction, before returning to their places right before grandpa appears.

<img src="covers/2101376938258624640.jpg" alt="Image 1" width="480" />

**Prompt**

```text
【Reference Image Usage Rules】

@image1 is the starting frame of this film. Strictly inherit its composition, camera angle, lighting, color tone, and the spatial relationships of all objects.

【Starting Frame Content】A thick sketchbook lies open on a dark wooden desk; the pages are yellowed, and the paper surface has a delicate fibrous texture. On the left page is a samurai drawn in blue colored pencil with extremely dense, scribbled, and repeatedly layered lines—posed in a profile lunge, holding an unsheathed katana with both hands, the blade tip pointing to the right; the figure is surrounded by messy cross-hatching and smudge marks, with blue handwritten Japanese calligraphy on the left side of the page and a blue starburst drawn in the upper left corner. On the right page is a samurai drawn in red colored pencil in the exact same style—standing in a mirrored lunge confrontation stance against the blue samurai, holding a sword with both hands, the blade tip pointing to the left, with the tips of both swords nearly touching at the gutter of the book; a large red kanji character "侍" (samurai) is written in the upper right corner of the page, with red handwritten script in the upper left corner.

A blue colored pencil lies diagonally below the left page, and a red colored pencil lies diagonally below the right page, both pencil tips pointing toward the center of the book.

【Background Decor】Arranged in sequence in the dim background behind the desk are: a beige coarse-ceramic brush pot with black ink calligraphy inscribed on its body, resting atop a circular wooden coaster; a dark metal samurai figurine holding a spear in a majestic pose; a hanging calligraphy scroll mounted in a dark wooden frame, displaying a giant black character "魂" (soul) on white paper, stamped with a red seal below; an old dark brown leather trunk with brass clasps; a wooden brush pot holding a row of ink brushes and paintbrushes, bristles facing upward; several stacked vintage books with red and white labels on their spines; a pair of black-rimmed glasses casually set on the desk; and several miscellaneous small items scattered across the desktop.

【Lighting】The entire room is in low illumination. A warm beam of side light enters diagonally from the upper left of the frame, illuminating the open pages and the two colored pencils, while the background furnishings fade into soft shadows. Tiny specks of dust drift gently within the light beam.

Frame 0 must be completely identical to @image1; motion begins naturally from here. Recomposing, changing camera angles, altering lighting, or reversing motion is strictly prohibited.

【Characters: @bluesamurai and @redsamurai】

Both characters are entirely composed of colored pencil lines, sketches brought to life directly from the sketchbook pages.

@bluesamurai: Entire body consists of dense, scribbly, repeatedly layered blue colored pencil lines, without any solid skin, fabric, or metallic textures—he is literally a cluster of living, moving pencil strokes. His outline depicts a Japanese samurai: topknot hair, broad shoulders, kimono top and hakama pants, bare feet, holding a katana formed entirely from blue lines with both hands. His line edges are rough and discontinuous; when in motion, the lines jitter slightly as if blown by the wind, recombining and leaving behind faint trailing pencil strokes.

@redsamurai: Identical in structure to @bluesamurai, but his entire body is composed of red colored pencil lines, wielding a katana made of red lines.

【Scale & Dimensions · Strict Compliance Required】Both figures are only as large as drawn in the sketchbook—roughly the length of a colored pencil, extremely miniature relative to everyday desktop objects like the desk, brush pots, books, and glasses. The top of their heads is only a few times the thickness of that blue colored pencil.

They must absolutely never enlarge, never become life-sized, and never exceed their proportions relative to the desktop objects. Throughout the fight, surrounding brush pots, books, statues, and trunks should loom over them like massive architectural structures.

【Destruction Logic · Strict Compliance Required】Because they are miniature, destruction in the room is never caused by brute force, but rather through extreme speed and chain reactions: stepping on, knocking over, slicing, pushing off, toppling, and triggering domino-like collapses.

Blade edges can slice through knotted cords, slash paper surfaces, and cut through brush handles; impacts can tip brush pots, knock over book stacks, and tilt photo frames; landing impacts can jolt small desktop items into the air. One object falling knocks over the next.

It is strictly prohibited for them to smash furniture like giants. All destruction must strictly obey the physics of "two miniature beings darting across a desk at breakneck speeds."

【Global Art Style】

Mixed rendering where two distinct textures must coexist clearly:

@bluesamurai and @redsamurai are pure colored pencil sketches—two-dimensional, linear, with paper-grain pencil strokes; they must never become realistic 3D figures, and must never develop skin or cloth textures. Even when leaping through the air off the paper, they remain a dynamic cluster of colored pencil strokes.

The desk, the room, and all objects possess extremely photorealistic live-action photography quality: deep wood grains, ceramic glazes, metallic glints, paper fibers, leather wear, and dust floating in sunbeams—all photorealistic.

The integration of both must be seamless: the sketched characters cast faint shadows onto the real desk surface, and their strokes leave brief colored pencil scuffs on the paper and wood as they move.

ARRI Alexa 35 cinematic aesthetic, high dynamic range, 2.39:1 widescreen aspect ratio, shallow depth of field, subtle 35mm film grain.

【Dialogue Rules】

This film contains zero dialogue. Absolutely no spoken lines, no narration, no internal monologues throughout. All text in this prompt consists solely of visual scene instructions and must never be read aloud or voiced.

Audio only consists of: the rustling of turning sketchbook pages, the scratching sound of colored pencils gliding across paper, tiny crisp clangs of metallic blades, the clatter of objects tumbling and rolling, the crack of ceramic shattering, the tearing of paper, the snapping of brush shafts, and finally the distant creak of a door hinge and approaching footsteps.

【Camera Direction Principles】

Continuous single take throughout; hard cuts, editing jumps, black screen transitions, and freeze-frames are strictly prohibited.

Macro cinematography, with the camera maintained at desk level or slightly above, like a miniature camera navigating the desktop surface. Frequent use of low-angle tracking close to the desk surface, high-speed horizontal tracking along the tabletop, orbiting around objects, and rapid vertical tilts tracking characters as they leap onto brush pots.

Shallow depth of field: when tracking characters, background decor remains softly blurred; only the characters and the specific object they are interacting with or destabilizing remain sharply in focus.

The camera may only navigate through physically existing open gaps, never clipping through objects, book pages, or the desktop.

【Choreography Density Requirements】

Between 0:06 and 0:24, there must be 4–6 distinct changes in offensive/defensive state every single second. Taking turns, striking and pausing, waiting for the opponent, or choreographed alternating performances are strictly forbidden. The tempo is blazingly fast yet not artificially fast-forwarded, without motion blurs concealing the action—every sword swing must feature a distinct wind-up, trajectory, and recovery.

【0.0—3.0s | The Breeze】

The visual matches @image1 exactly. A light breeze blows in from off-camera.

The edges of the open sketchbook pages begin to flutter gently, lifting and settling back down. Dust motes in the sunbeam are blown diagonally. The red colored pencil rolls ever so slightly on the paper by a few millimeters.

The wind ceases. Everything falls dead silent for half a second.

Then—@bluesamurai's lines stir. The blue hatchings forming his arm softly rearrange themselves. Immediately following, the lines of @redsamurai's sword-gripping fingers twitch and move.

Camera: Extremely slow push-in from @image1's camera position toward the center gutter of the book, depth of field shallowing accordingly.

【3.0—6.0s | Coming to Life】

Both sketches peel themselves off the paper simultaneously—their lines rise, curl, and gain dimensional thickness from the flat 2D page, yet remain pure pencil strokes. Their original resting positions on the paper are left behind: two blank paper silhouettes surrounded only by residual messy cross-hatching.

@bluesamurai stands upright, the lines of his shoulders loosening and expanding. @redsamurai flicks his sword, red lines trailing a lingering stroke in the air that promptly disperses.

The two lock eyes across the centerfold of the sketchbook.

Then, they simultaneously sprint forward. The paper beneath their feet crumples with micro-creases and colored pencil skids.

Camera: Low-angle skimming the paper surface, shooting upward from the gutter at the two rising figures, rapidly pulling backward along the centerfold the moment they launch into motion.

【6.0—11.0s | Clash on the Pages】

Their swords collide right in the center of the book with a tiny, crisp metallic ring; the point of impact bursts with a tiny puff of colored pencil dust—half blue, half red.

They clash at high speed across the spread: lunges, parries, downward presses, horizontal slashes, backward leaps, springboard steps off the paper, rolls. Every footstep leaves a tiny color smudge on the page, and every swing trails a fleeting colored pencil streak in the air.

@redsamurai executes a wide horizontal slash that misses, the blade edge carving across the paper—slicing a long tear into the sheet, the paper edge curling upward.

@bluesamurai leaps off the curled paper edge, executing a downward slash from above; @redsamurai raises his blade horizontally to block, forced down to one knee into the crumples of the paper by the sheer downward momentum.

The nearby blue colored pencil rolls half a turn from the vibrations of their clash.

Camera: Skimming the paper surface tracking their footwork across the book, tilting up rapidly with the jump, plunging swiftly with the downward strike.

【11.0—16.0s | Leaving the Book】

@redsamurai kicks off against the pressing blade, using the recoil to backflip off the pages and land onto the adjacent wooden desk surface. @bluesamurai immediately gives chase.

The battlefield shifts to the tabletop. They weave through a pair of black-rimmed glasses—@redsamurai steps onto the spectacle temple arm to springboard off, kicking the glasses over onto their other side, sending them sliding half a foot away.

@bluesamurai sprints low along the desk surface, thrusting forward; @redsamurai sidesteps, and the blue blade plunges into the wood grain, kicking up a tiny speck of wood shavings as it pulls free.

Trading blows, they crash into the stack of vintage books—the top book slides off from the impact, hitting the desk with a dull thud, unsettling the two books beneath it and sending their pages fluttering wildly.

Camera: Tracking over the edge of the book as they leap off, then skimming laterally across the desk surface, reacting with a subtle jolt and recoil as the book stack falls.

【16.0—21.0s | Chain Collapse】

@redsamurai dashes along the scattered book spines and leaps onto the rim of the beige coarse-ceramic brush pot. @bluesamurai kicks off the round wooden coaster to pursue.

The two clash right along the pot's rim—blade arcs flashing across the razor-thin circular edge. A sweeping slash from @bluesamurai forces @redsamurai back; his foot slips, and he catches the rim with one hand to balance himself, but the sudden force rocks the entire ceramic pot.

The brush pot wobbles twice and topples over.

It rolls off the wooden coaster, colliding into the adjacent wooden brush pot; ink brushes clatter and spill everywhere, shafts rolling across the tabletop. One brush slides down and strikes the brass clasp of the old leather trunk, popping the lid open.

@bluesamurai hops between the rolling brush shafts while @redsamurai keeps his footing atop the rolling ceramic pot, their blades trading strikes without pause.

Camera: Rapid ascent following their leap onto the brush pot, tilting and plunging downward as the pot topples, then tracking along the desk surface tracing the path of the domino collapses.

【21.0—24.5s | Peak Density】

Combat density ramps to the highest intensity of the entire film.

The two trade lightning-fast blows amidst complete chaos: rolling brush shafts, splayed book pages, overturned brush pots, and the popped-open leather trunk. 5 to 7 offensive-defensive transitions per second—thrust, parry, deflect, slash, redirect, step, flip, shoulder check.

@redsamurai kicks off the base of the metal samurai statue to launch into the air, jarring the statue sideways; @bluesamurai executes a mid-air downward slash, blade grazing the frame of the hanging "魂" (soul) scroll—the frame jolts askew, sliding off its stand to lean against the wall.

A small trinket on the desk gets kicked flying, bounces off the desk rim, and tumbles into the darkness below.

Camera: Ultra-tight orbit circling the two closely, points of blade contact consistently sharp, while the chaotic background blurs into abstract swatches of color within the shallow depth of field.

【24.5—26.5s | The Door Creaks】

From the distance comes the creak of a wooden door pushing open, followed by the sound of slippered footsteps stepping onto wooden floorboards, steadily approaching.

Both freeze mid-air simultaneously—sword tips locked against each other, both pairs of sketched line-eyes swiveling toward the door.

Silence for half a second.

Then, both instantly disengage, sprinting madly back toward the sketchbook.

Camera: Freezes dead in its tracks the instant the door sounds, steadily locked on the two frozen mid-air, then whips into a high-speed tracking shot following their dash back to the book.

【26.5—28.5s | Returning to Place】

Both leap back onto the open sketchbook pages.

@bluesamurai rushes into the left page, @redsamurai slides into the right page, each sliding back into the two blank silhouettes—their lines collapse flat, spread out, and melt back into the paper fibers, returning to their original lunging sword-drawn confrontation poses.

The very last quivering red line settles into absolute stillness.

Nothing remains on the pages except two static drawings. Only the sword tear remains in the paper, its frayed edge still slightly curled.

Camera: Rapidly drops from their running height down to a paper-skimming angle, stabilizing on them the moment they collapse flat into the page, then slowly dollies upward.

【28.5—30.0s | Grandpa】

A figure appears at the edge of the frame—an elderly Japanese man wearing plain traditional Japanese loungewear, with short grizzled gray hair, a deeply wrinkled face, holding a steaming teacup in both hands.

He stops at the doorway, looking up.

The camera pans with his gaze across the entire room: the overturned ceramic brush pot, ink brushes scattered all over the desk, strewn books, the tilted "魂" calligraphy scroll, the open leather trunk, the flipped spectacles, and total disarray across the desk.

The old man's eyes slowly widen. His mouth parts slightly in disbelief. The teacup remains frozen in his grip as steam continues to drift upward.

His gaze lands on the sketchbook lying open on the desk—showing only two quiet, motionless colored pencil sketches.

Final frame: The elderly man stands in the doorway, holding his teacup, dumbfounded by the room's total mess; the sketchbook on the desk lies silently open, blue samurai on the left, red samurai on the right, the tips of their swords meeting motionless at the gutter. The shot concludes naturally.

Camera: Slowly pans from the desk toward the old man at the doorway, sweeps with his gaze across the ruined room, returns finally to the quiet sketchbook, and slowly dollies out to a wide shot. No freeze frame, no posed freeze—steam from the teacup continues to rise, and the torn paper edge still trembles faintly.

【Global Constraints & Anti-Degradation Rules】

masterpiece, highest quality. Mixed rendering: pure colored pencil line characters + photorealistic room.

@bluesamurai is exclusively composed of blue colored pencil strokes throughout; @redsamurai is exclusively composed of red colored pencil strokes throughout. Their colors must never swap, mix, solidify into textured skin or fabric, or turn into realistic 3D human models.

Both characters' physical size must remain strictly as small as originally drawn in the sketchbook, never enlarging or becoming life-sized.

Only two swords exist throughout the entire sequence, both made purely of sketched lines. Weapon duplication or size drifting is strictly prohibited.
```

```text
Create an original GTA-style 3D car chase following the production workflow below.

[Design]
Establish one main driver, one getaway vehicle, one pursuing vehicle, and one urban setting, maintaining design consistency across all elements. Plan a total of 3 shots, each lasting 4 seconds:
- A chase shot following the vehicles from behind
- A side tracking shot of the vehicles navigating a sharp turn
- A wide establishing shot capturing the vehicles speeding away

[Build in Blender]
Create clean greybox models along with functional character and vehicle rigs. Texturing and UV unwrapping are not required.

[Animation and Testing]
Animate the driver, steering wheel manipulation, wheel rotation, vehicles, and cameras. Maintain consistent travel direction and front-to-back vehicle relationships, and resolve issues such as clipping, floating wheels, unnatural tire slippage, collapsing poses, and hands detaching from the steering wheel.

[Render in Blender]
Render frames 1 to 288 at 1280x720 and 24fps. Assemble the actual Blender-rendered frames into a 12-second master greybox video. Export each shot separately, and also render corresponding greybox still frames to serve as shape and composition references.

[Finishing with PixVerse Plugin]
Process each shot individually using Seedance 2.5 at 720p. Use the Blender video clips as motion references and the greybox still frames as shape references. [Review and Delivery]
Review both finished videos, checking for visual glitches and continuity across shots. Fix any Blender-side issues and regenerate only the shots that failed in Seedance. Limit retries to a maximum of 2 attempts per shot.
```

<img src="covers/2098326362801221835.jpg" alt="Image 1" width="480" />

**Prompt**

```text
[Global Settings]
30 seconds, 16:9, 4K resolution target, 24fps, semi-realistic stylized 3D CG, bleak snowy fantasy wuxia cinematic aesthetic. Finely animated characters combined with physically realistic 3D textures: subsurface scattering on skin, fabric weave, worn leather, metallic reflections, distinct inertia in hair strands and clothing drape. Characters have realistic weight; slashes are driven by pushing off the ground, pivoting hips, and twisting the torso, with clothing and hair violently whipping during high-speed directional shifts. A blue-gray snowy landscape and cold white rim backlight unify the characters and environment, while dual-colored blade auras briefly illuminate faces, wooden pillars, and drifting snow.
Not a gentle back-and-forth exchange, not circling in place. Core combat loop: Explosive close-in → dense attack and defense → heavy strike forcing knockback → immediate pursuit → clashing again at a new landing point. A forces a breakthrough, B repeatedly contests positioning to cut off the route and counterattacks fiercely; the fight continuously cuts past boulders, a paifang archway, and tree roots. Ultimately, A wins the passage behind the gate while B remains in hot pursuit.

Non-stop high-speed combat from 0 to 30 seconds at normal time speed, free of slow motion, slow camera moves, bullet time, hit-stops, or freeze frames. Clashing already in the first frame, still trading slashes in rapid advance at the final frame. No independent skill activations, charging, magic arrays, or summons; every weapon swing inevitably carries a blade aura that unfurls along the edge; heavy impact camera shakes must occur after actual physical contact.

[Asset References and Lock-in]
@Image 1 : A, adult black-haired swordswoman, strictly following the facial identity, high ponytail, red hair ribbon, red-and-black short combat outfit, long bracers, leg armor, and black tall boots from the reference image. Wields the curved-blade single long saber from the reference image, unsheathed from the very first frame, right hand as primary grip with left hand providing support; empty scabbard strapped to the back. Vermilion fluid blade aura with an orange-gold blade edge. Combat style leans toward aggressive assault, breaking through defensive lines with lunging blade presses and returning heavy cuts.

@Image 2 : B, adult blonde swordswoman, strictly following the facial identity, long pale-blonde hair, blue hair ornament, blue-and-black patterned dress with white lace trim, wrist guards, white stockings, and black shoes from the reference image. Wields a curved-blade long saber in each hand from the reference image; cyan-silver fluid blade aura with a silver-white blade edge and a deep cyan outer contour. Combat style leans toward high-speed interception, parrying with one blade while immediately counter-cutting with the other, repositioning diagonally to re-block the route.
@Image 3 : A massive, weathered wooden paifang archway entwined with an ancient gnarled tree in a snow-covered canyon. Lock in the modeling, textures, and spatial relationships of the boulders, paifang, gate posts, tree canopy, thick exposed roots, and icicles beneath the eaves. Use only the environment; do not retain the distant small figures or the top/bottom black bars from the image.

Only A and B appear throughout the entire clip, with three blades in total. A always wields one blade, B always wields dual blades; no face swaps, no outfit changes, no weapon swapping, no duplicate clones, and no repeated asset references in the prompt body. Snow gradually accumulates on their shoe soles and hem skirts; subtle wear marks progressively appear on armor and blade surfaces, and battle damage does not reset.

[Scene and Spatial Trajectory]
Snow squalls sweep across a blue-gray canyon, where a colossal ancient tree twines around a dilapidated paifang. Thick branches are blanketed in snow, and icicles dangle under the wooden eaves. Distant cold mist is preserved, while the foreground combat zone remains crisp and clear, ensuring characters are never swallowed by the fog.

In the foreground lies an open snowfield; the inner flank of the boulder on the left connects to the left portal of the paifang. Fleshing out the space behind the mist in the reference image: behind the portal lies a wide root path extending along the left flank of the ancient tree, lined with low, step-able root shoulders; the end of this path bypasses a horizontal root, connecting to a gentle downhill snow slope behind the gate. Weathered low wooden railings stand along the gate's side, with snow-laden withered branches above the horizontal root, leaving the path unblocked.

The fight advances continuously along the route: "Foreground snowfield → inner flank of the left boulder → left portal of the paifang → root path along the ancient tree's left side → snow slope behind the gate", never looping back to the starting point. The paifang and ancient tree gradually shift from front to side, then recede behind them, serving as authentic spatial markers of displacement.
Destruction accumulates along the path: ice crusts shatter off boulder surfaces, side railings snap on impact, eaves icicles shatter and plummet, withered branches beside the root path snap, and continuous skid trenches carve into the ground. The main paifang structure and ancient tree trunk remain structurally sound; destroyed objects do not restore.

[VFX and Camera Work]
50% fluid + 25% particles + 25% snow/ice medium: Vermilion/orange-gold and cyan-silver fluid blade auras thickly envelop the cutting edges, bright particles spray along the direction of each swing, and snow powder, ice chips, and chilled mist erupt alongside footwork, blade paths, and collisions.

Horizontal sweeps form wide fan arcs, upward cuts form rising crescents, and heavy downward cleaves create thick wedge-shaped blade lights; visual effects tightly follow the authentic blade trajectory without homing or expanding into ranged ultimate abilities. Short strikes still exhibit clean blade auras; sparks merely accentuate impact rhythm without replacing blade light. Cyan-silver blade auras preserve deep cyan silhouettes so they do not blend into the white snow backdrop; the two colors briefly flare together only at intersection points, never tinting the entire screen.

Heavy strikes first show clear weapon contact, followed by a brief directional camera shake, immediately pulling back along the direction of the knockback; time does not stop, characters and debris keep moving. Light clashes produce no camera shake; no black-and-white impact frames. Motion blur is concentrated on the environment and moving edges, keeping key collision points, faces, and weapon grips sharp and readable.

Pseudo-one-shot technique, written in 3-second blocks with second-by-second descriptions; transitions achieved via rapid push-ins, whip pans, sudden rises/descents, and close passes by rocks and wooden pillars. Close-up combat sticks tight, rapid knockbacks immediately pull wide, and pursuits close the distance again at high velocity; no prolonged constant-speed side tracking, no arbitrary roll rotations. Wide shots must clearly show the origin, trajectory of the knockback, and new landing spot, never substituting background swaps for physical displacement.
Each short burst of engagement features a combined 8–10 tight, active moves between both fighters, rather than artificially squeezing ten weapon swings into long knockbacks and pursuits. Pure melee weapon combat without punches or kicks. After every impact-driven displacement, the pursuer instantly follows up, and the opponent counters immediately upon landing without waiting for the snow and smoke to settle.

[Shot 1 (0–3s) | Crashing Clash in the Snow]
t=1s: Camera skims low over the snow surface rushing forward at extreme speed; FS full-shot low-angle: A, at the front-left, is already bounding across the snow toward B at the back-right, driving a diagonal cleave into B's right blade. B's left blade immediately counters with a return slash; A parries it away and continues pressing forward. Vermilion and cyan-silver blade auras cross and unfurl, kicking twin sprays of snow powder outward from behind their heels.
t=2s: Camera whips tightly along the clash point toward the left boulder; MS medium shot: A's horizontal slash forces B's right blade to parry; A's upward flick clashes against B's downward pressing left blade, followed by a diagonal cleave forcing B to alternate guard blocks. Seizing an opening during A's recovery, B slashes toward A's weapon-holding side; A pulls back her shoulder, parries, and both sprint along a diagonal vector as dense sparks burst intermittently between thick blade arcs.
t=3s: Camera pulls back sharply into a wide full shot along B's retreat direction; A pushes off the ground, pivots her hips, and executes a two-handed heavy cleave slamming into B's crossed dual blades. ### A woman searches for someone in the middle of Shibuya Scramble — a 15-second live-action urban melodrama with step-printed motion blur trails on the surrounding crowd and city.
```

Author：[@magic\_ai\_skill](https://x.com/magic_ai_skill) · [Source](https://x.com/magic_ai_skill/status/2102289833326772324)

Character · Cityscape / Street · Published

**Summary:** A woman searches for someone in the middle of Shibuya Scramble — a 15-second live-action urban melodrama with step-printed motion blur trails on the surrounding crowd and city.

<img src="covers/2102289833326772324.jpg" alt="Image 1" width="480" />

**Prompt**

```text
A woman searches for someone in the middle of Shibuya Scramble — a 15-second live-action urban melodrama. She moves naturally while step-printed trails flow through the surrounding city and crowd.

[SEQUENCE]
(0:00~0:06) A 35mm handheld medium-wide tracking shot follows beside and behind the woman at her running speed. From the first frame, wearing her brown leather jacket, she runs from the near curb into the crowd on the green pedestrian signal. Keep her running body visible from head to feet as she swings her arms and takes long strides. Her grey wide-leg trousers move with her legs and her ponytail kicks behind her. After passing the first group, she continues through successive gaps in another crossing group and the people beyond it. Shoulders and clothing pass on both sides of her and fall behind as new pedestrians approach ahead. The camera shows both her running body and the route through the crowd, following without a cut for six seconds until she reaches the crossing's centre. Her movement remains continuous; every moving pedestrian around her, near and far, carries overlapping motion traces and painterly colour smears.
(0:06~0:09) Hard cut to an external front three-quarter view of the woman arriving at the crossing's centre, a 50mm handheld medium shot. In her brown leather jacket, she shortens her strides and stops, turning left before urgently checking the crowd to her right. Not finding the face she wants, she draws her brows together and parts her lips. Her head and expressions move continuously, keeping her searching eyes readable. Every pedestrian passing around her drags trails along their own direction of travel.
(0:09~0:12) Remaining near the centre in her grey wide-leg trousers, she makes a half-turn to search the people she has just passed. Her gaze settles as though she recognises someone, then she hesitates and searches another face. A 35mm medium arc tracking shot moves sideways along a short arc from a front three-quarter view. Nearby pedestrians sweep past as broad strokes of coloured paint, with the same trailing effect continuing through the distant crowd. Building facades and billboards leave long coloured trails aligned with the camera movement. She naturally turns her body and head near the centre of the frame; her expression and head outline remain distinct from the surrounding smears.
(0:12~0:15) In her brown leather jacket, she scans between receding pedestrians. She starts a step in one direction, hesitates, then exhales and looks aside. A 50mm medium close-up with gentle lateral movement. Her face and head movement remain readable while the entire surrounding crowd and illuminated signs flow like wet brushstrokes. End during a searching glance, with nobody found.

[CHARACTER]
One beautiful East Asian adult woman in her early twenties, with a slim oval face, brown almond-shaped eyes and natural makeup. Her long black hair is tied in a high ponytail, with loose strands framing her face. She wears small silver earrings, a dark brown cropped leather jacket, a charcoal high-neck top, grey wide-leg cargo trousers and black ankle boots. Keep her face, build, hairstyle and outfit consistent across all shots. No greeting to camera or fashion posing. Urgency alternates with hesitation, without screaming or sobbing.

[SETTING]
Shibuya Scramble at night: white diagonal crossing stripes, pedestrians from several directions, large illuminated billboards and shopfronts. She runs from the near curb to the centre, then stays near that centre through the ending. Pedestrian signals remain green throughout; vehicles wait behind their stop lines. People cross naturally at different speeds, passing around her rather than through her.

[CINEMATIC LOOK]
A lonely, restless urban melodrama. Visible 35mm film grain, deep night blacks, teal and amber billboard light brushing real skin and brown leather. Skin and clothing remain photographic; the smearing is a camera effect.

[STEP PRINTING]
Concentrate the effect on the world around her. The woman's movement and the camera's progress retain continuous real-time motion. Briefly repeated motion traces overlap with long exposure trails across the surrounding crowd, flowing like wet paint. Keep the repetition intervals short and the trails connected: surrounding time seems to drift and smear rather than the entire image freezing and jumping. Apply the same treatment to every moving pedestrian in the foreground, middle ground and background, with trail lengths corresponding to their movement. Buildings retain their shape and position; camera movement creates directional trails from facade details and billboard lights. Keep only natural short motion blur on the woman's face and body, allowing her expressions and head turns to flow smoothly.

[CAMERA]
Film the woman from an external viewpoint throughout. For the first six seconds, track beside and behind her, showing her full running body and her passage through the crowd together. Continue with a front three-quarter view at the centre, a short arc movement and a closer facial view. Hard cuts at 6, 9 and 12 seconds preserve the same crossing and arrival position. Camera movement within each shot remains continuous.

[LIGHTING]
Billboards, shopfronts and streetlights provide the illumination. Existing light reveals her face while deep shadows remain between buildings and behind the crowd. Keep it recognisably night.

[COMPOSITION]
In the opening shot, centre her full running body in the middle ground, showing the gaps ahead and pedestrians passing on both sides and falling behind her. Continue to centre her as pedestrians cross the foreground and background. Her searching eyes and hesitant expression remain readable amid the surrounding colour trails.

[BGM]
A continuous urban-film score combining loneliness with restless urgency: a low recurring pulse and an unresolved melodic phrase, with no emotional resolution at the end. Neither cheerful travel-vlog music nor a horror chase score.

[DIALOGUE]
No dialogue or voice-over.

[LOCATION SOUND]
Footsteps, close breathing, distant crowd wash and pedestrian-crossing tones sit beneath the music. Sound flows continuously.

[PRECAUTIONS]
Trails are temporal traces of the same people, not additional cloned people. Buildings retain their shape and position; camera movement creates long exposure trails from their lights and facade edges. No subtitles, logos, watermarks or explanatory overlays.

[FINISH]
Preserve her identity, outfit, real skin and night palette. Inner conflict is visible in searching, hesitating and searching again. Hard cut at 15 seconds during movement, without a resolution or frozen end card.
```

Author：[@YourAlphaMom](https://x.com/YourAlphaMom) · [Source](https://x.com/YourAlphaMom/status/2102122125901328606)

**Summary:** Handheld smartphone-style footage of fishermen hauling a net aboard a boat, revealing a live mermaid who lifts herself up and startles the crew.

<img src="covers/2102122125901328606.jpg" alt="Image 1" width="480" />

**Prompt**

```text
Create a 15-second, vertical 9:16 ultra-realistic video of fishermen hauling an enormous mermaid aboard a working fishing boat. ONE continuous handheld shot, recorded by an unseen crew member standing on the deck.

CAMERA AND LIGHTING

Authentic casual smartphone footage: slight hand shake, imperfect framing, ordinary autofocus, natural motion blur, and realistic digital zoom. The recording device and its operator remain out of frame.

Bright overcast daylight. Neutral colours, soft shadows, wet surfaces, worn fishing equipment, and an ordinary grey sea. Natural skin and scale texture, without cinematic lighting or beauty filters.

The observer stays several steps inside the deck throughout, with a clear view of the net, both fishermen, and the creature. The boat rocks gently.

SETTING AND CREATURE

A working fishing boat with a broad, wet deck, a metal gunwale, and a small hydraulic lifting boom. Two fishermen wear waterproof jackets, rubber gloves, and work boots.

A heavy fishing net hangs from the boom above the deck immediately inside the gunwale. The winch supports its weight; the fishermen guide and open it.

Inside is a roughly three-metre-long mermaid with the appearance of an exceptionally beautiful 24-year-old woman: a striking feminine face, expressive eyes, full lips, pale skin, long tangled dark hair, human arms, a slim waist, a very curvy figure, a very large full bust, and rounded hips flowing naturally into a thick grey-green fish tail with a broad fin. She is strikingly sexy, with soft, naturally full curves and believable anatomy. Dense overlapping scales cover her chest and continue down into the tail. Her appearance is organic and lifelike, with irregular scales, saltwater, and small natural skin imperfections.

Her torso and tail form one continuous body. She has two arms and a single tail.

CONTINUOUS ACTION

Start MID-HAUL. The dripping net is already being brought aboard, suspended just above the deck. Its load fills much of the frame.

An enormous fish tail protrudes through the loose opening. Beside it, between folds of netting, a pale human hand is clearly visible. Her face is still concealed by the net and wet hair.

The unseen person filming reacts immediately:

“What the hell did you catch?”

The winch lowers the net onto the clear deck. Water drains across the boards. The creature’s weight visibly settles onto the surface before the fishermen loosen the net.

One fisherman pulls the slack mesh away from her upper body while the other draws it clear of her tail. The creature remains in the same position as the net opens around her.

The view makes a short, slightly uneven digital zoom toward the human hand, then adjusts upward to reveal her face beneath the wet hair. Her eyes are open. She slowly turns them toward the nearest fisherman.

Hold this discovery briefly, with audible breathing from behind the camera.

Then she abruptly plants both palms on the deck and lifts her upper body. Her heavy tail flexes and SLAPS the wet deck once, throwing a sheet of water toward the fishermen.

Both men recoil in separate, instinctive movements, releasing the loose mesh and stepping away.

The person filming shouts:

“It’s moving!”

The view quickly zooms back out to keep her and the retreating fishermen visible. A brief startled shake interrupts the framing, but the creature remains readable.

End at 15 seconds with her upper body raised on her arms, hair hanging around her face, looking toward the person filming. The loosened net remains beneath and beside her.

SOUND AND CONTINUITY

Natural live audio only: winch motor, water draining, quiet boat engine, wind, wet netting, boots on deck, the heavy tail slap, startled breathing, and the two short English reactions. No music or added horror sound effects.

The net visibly opens to reveal the same creature already inside it. Preserve her body, the fishermen, equipment, and deck layout throughout.

One uninterrupted recording at normal speed. No cuts, hidden transitions, slow motion, or external viewpoints.

No subtitles, graphics, watermark, or recording interface.
```

**Summary:** Create a 15-second, vertical 9:16 ultra-realistic motorcycle stunt video filmed in one continuous handheld shot from a moving car, featuring a blonde female stunt rider jumping between mountain ramps and a flatbed truck.

**Prompt**

```text
Create a 15-second, vertical 9:16 ultra-realistic motorcycle stunt video filmed in ONE continuous handheld shot by an unseen passenger in a moving car.

The image must feel like genuine smartphone footage: natural hand shake, subtle vehicle vibration, slightly imperfect framing, realistic motion blur, and small autofocus and exposure adjustments. The entire action unfolds continuously in front of one physical camera.

MAIN SUBJECT

An exceptionally beautiful 24-year-old platinum-blonde woman with a striking feminine face, a slim waist, a very curvy hourglass figure, a very large full bust, rounded hips, full rounded buttocks, and long shapely legs. Soft, naturally full curves, believable anatomy, body weight, and movement.

She wears a fitted red-and-black motorcycle suit that closely follows her figure, black riding gloves, matching motorcycle boots, and a motorcycle helmet with a clear visor. Her long blonde hair is secured in a low ponytail beneath the helmet.

She rides one red-and-black supermoto motorcycle with a narrow saddle, long-travel suspension, exposed mechanical details, and two road tyres. Preserve the same woman, outfit, helmet, motorcycle, and proportions throughout.

LOCATION AND SPATIAL CONTINUITY

A broad mountain road runs beneath a dramatic rocky mountainside. A separate access road runs alongside it, giving the filming passenger an unobstructed view across the action.

An enormous launch ramp extends from an elevated terrace on the mountainside toward the main road. Its riding surface, steel supports, and upward-curving launch lip are clearly visible. The ramp points forward along the road, so her first jump carries her into the direction of traffic.

Two passenger cars travel ahead in staggered positions, leaving a continuous open route between and around them.

Farther ahead, a conventional flatbed recovery truck is parked facing the same direction. It has a recognizable driver’s cab, three axles, large tyres, side mirrors, amber roof beacons, and a steel recovery bed.

A purpose-built stunt ramp is securely mounted on its bed. The ramp begins on the asphalt behind the truck, climbs over the bed, and ends at a raised launch lip above the cab roof. Its supporting framework is visible. The motorcycle’s departure path clears the cab.

The mountainside ramp, road, cars, recovery truck, and second ramp are already present from the beginning. Their positions remain consistent.

CAMERA

The unseen passenger records through the open side window of a car travelling along the parallel access road.

Begin with an oblique side view showing the woman’s final approach along the mountain ramp and the road below. The viewpoint moves forward with the filming car while following her action.

After her first landing, use ONE smooth, continuous zoom to bring the motorcycle and her overtakes closer. Keep enough surrounding road visible to understand her path.

Maintain that zoom through the truck approach and second jump. Track her with natural handheld panning. The camera remains inside the same car throughout.

The recording device and its operator never appear.

CONTINUOUS ACTION

Start immediately with her already accelerating along the final section of the huge mountain ramp. Her full body and motorcycle are visible. She reaches the lip and launches within the first TWO seconds.

Both wheels leave the ramp. She remains on the motorcycle as it travels forward and downward toward the road. The view follows the entire airborne trajectory, keeping the landing area readable.

She lands on a clear stretch of asphalt behind the travelling cars. Show the tyres making contact and the suspension compressing and recovering. The motorcycle continues rolling forward immediately.

Only after the landing does the continuous zoom begin.

She rapidly closes the distance to the two cars and passes them through the available gaps. Her route forms two flowing bends: a clear lean in one direction, followed by a lean in the other, then a return upright. The motorcycle follows those curves through tyre contact with the road.

The cars continue along their lanes. Their movement remains independent and predictable while she passes.

The parked recovery truck and its rear ramp grow larger ahead. She finishes the overtakes and straightens the motorcycle into alignment with the ramp before reaching it.

She rides directly onto the ramp from the asphalt behind the truck. Show the front wheel entering first, followed by the rear wheel. Both wheels follow the same solid inclined surface as she climbs above the recovery bed.

The truck remains stationary. Its ramp keeps the same shape and angle.

At the raised lip, the front wheel leaves first and the rear wheel follows. She launches forward OVER the cab with clear space between the motorcycle and the cab roof.

The view follows the second jump in the same uninterrupted movement. Keep the truck beneath and behind her long enough to make the clearance unmistakable.

She descends onto the open road beyond the truck, lands with visible suspension compression, and continues riding forward. End at 15 seconds with the motorcycle still moving.

LIGHTING AND SOUND

Bright overcast daylight, a pale cloudy sky, neutral colours, soft natural shadows, realistic skin tones, and readable mechanical details. Ordinary phone exposure throughout.

Natural recorded audio only: motorcycle engine, wind passing the filming car, surrounding traffic, tyre contact, suspension movement, and the brief metallic rumble of wheels crossing the truck ramp.

The engine grows louder during the approaches and changes naturally with distance. No music, narration, or exaggerated impact effects.

ESSENTIAL CONTINUITY

One mountain-ramp launch, one road landing, two flowing overtakes, one uninterrupted climb up the truck ramp, one jump over the cab, and one final road landing.

One continuous shot at normal speed. No cuts, hidden transitions, slow motion, aerial views, or separate reaction shots.

Her hands remain on the handlebars and her boots remain on the foot pegs throughout the riding and jumps. Preserve natural suspension movement, rotating wheels, consistent scale, and continuous forward motion.

The motorcycle never becomes a different bike. The truck never changes its design or transforms into a ramp. Both ramps are physical structures with visible surfaces and supports.

Keep her and the motorcycle visible throughout both jumps. No camera teleportation, disappearing vehicles, midair direction changes, hovering, or reversed footage.

No captions, subtitles, logos, watermarks, or recording interface.
```

```text
OUTFIT & CONTINUITY

She wears the same outfit throughout the entire video:

Strict continuity: same woman, same face, same hairstyle, same makeup, same outfit, same accessories, and consistent body proportions throughout every shot.

LOCATION

A beautiful modern European city during an overcast rainy afternoon.

Environment includes:

- Small independent cafés
- Glass shop windows

- Wet pavement
- Street lights
- Trees moving gently in the breeze

STORYBOARD — 30 SECONDS / 10 CUTS

CUT 01 — 0:00–0:03 | STUCK INSIDE

Handheld selfie footage inside her apartment.

She stands beside a large apartment window, looking outside at the rain. She briefly turns the camera toward the rainy street, then back toward herself.

She makes a slightly unimpressed expression.

Dialogue:

She glances back toward the rain.

Natural facial expression, casual delivery, slight handheld shake.

CUT 02 — 0:03–0:06 | SPONTANEOUS DECISION

Quick natural jump cut.

She is now leaving the apartment building wearing her burgundy outfit and trench coat.

She looks into the camera with a playful, mischievous smile while walking toward the entrance.

Dialogue:

She steps outside into the rain without posing.

CUT 03 — 0:06–0:09 | RAINY STREET

Handheld walking selfie.

She walks along the wet European street. Small raindrops are visible on her hair and trench coat.

The camera is held slightly above eye level with natural movement.

A passing car creates a tiny splash near the pavement.

She laughs naturally.

Dialogue:
“Okay… maybe this was a bad idea.”

She smiles despite the rain.

CUT 04 — 0:09–0:12 | CITY DISCOVERY

She slows down while walking and notices something interesting across the street.

Switch to a brief first-person POV showing a charming independent bookstore or cozy café glowing warmly through rain-speckled glass.

Cut back to her face.

Her expression changes from bored to curious.

Dialogue:
“Oh… wait.”

CUT 05 — 0:12–0:15 | COZY DISCOVERY

Quick external angle from across the sidewalk.

She walks toward the cozy bookstore/café while holding her small shoulder bag.

She looks through the window and smiles.

Cut to a close handheld selfie as she says:

“I’ve never actually been in here.”

CUT 06 — 0:15–0:18 | INSIDE

She briefly picks up a book or looks at a small display.

No exaggerated acting.

Dialogue:
“Okay, this is actually really cute.”

CUT 07 — 0:18–0:21 | LITTLE MOMENT

Handheld close-up selfie.

She sits near the window with a warm drink.

Rainy streets are visible behind her through the glass.

A few loose strands of hair frame her face.

She takes a small sip, looks outside, then back at the camera with a satisfied smile.

No dialogue for a moment — just natural ambient café and rain sounds.

CUT 08 — 0:21–0:24 | RAIN CLEARS

She walks down the street while filming herself.

She looks upward and smiles.

CUT 09 — 0:24–0:27 | SUNSET PAYOFF

Wet pavement reflects the warm sky.

She turns toward the camera with a relaxed smile.

Her hair moves naturally in the breeze.

CUT 10 — 0:27–0:30 | FINAL SELFIE

FINAL STYLE RULE
```

Author：[@john\_my07](https://x.com/john_my07) · [Source](https://x.com/john_my07/status/2101690647313043576)

Cityscape / Street · Published

**Summary:** A 30-second documentary prompt reconstructing the construction of London Bridge and surrounding medieval street life around 1190-1200.

<img src="covers/2101690647313043576.jpg" alt="Image 1" width="480" />

**Prompt**

```text
Create a 30-second, 1080p, ultra-photorealistic historical documentary video using Seedance 2.5. Depict an ordinary day in London during the construction of the medieval stone London Bridge, approximately between 1176 and 1209. The video must look like genuine, naturally observed footage from that era, as if an invisible documentary camera had somehow captured real medieval life. No AI-generated appearance, no fantasy aesthetics, no overly polished cinematic visuals.

VIDEO SPECIFICATIONS:
Duration: 30 seconds
Resolution: 1080p
Aspect ratio: 16:9
Style: Authentic historical realism, observational documentary
Camera: Unseen camera operator, natural handheld movement
Lighting: Realistic daylight, natural atmospheric haze
Audio: Period-appropriate environmental sounds, workers, carts, river, birds, and human conversations. No modern music.

HISTORICAL SETTING:

London, England, during the construction of the first major medieval stone London Bridge. The year should feel approximately 1190–1200, with the bridge still incomplete. Use historically plausible medieval architecture, clothing, tools, construction methods, and daily life. The bridge is being constructed across the River Thames under the broader project begun in 1176 and completed in 1209.

The construction should visibly involve stone masonry, wooden scaffolding, timber piles, workers moving stone, ropes, hand tools, and boats transporting building materials. Avoid modern construction equipment, industrial machinery, cranes, safety helmets, modern roads, or anachronistic architecture.

SCENE 1 — 0–6 SECONDS: THE BRIDGE CONSTRUCTION

Open with a natural medium-wide view from the riverside, showing the partially constructed stone London Bridge dominating the frame. Several workers are actively building the bridge: masons shaping stone blocks with hand tools, laborers hauling materials, and workers standing on rough timber scaffolding. Wooden piles and temporary construction platforms extend into the Thames.

The camera moves slowly and naturally, as though an unseen person is observing the work from the riverbank. Workers behave independently, concentrating on their tasks. Some pause to communicate, while others carry stones and operate ropes. The unfinished arches and rough stone surfaces must look physically authentic and historically plausible.

The Thames flows naturally beneath the construction area, with wooden boats passing nearby. Dust, small stone fragments, wood textures, and subtle movement create a sense of a real working construction site.

SCENE 2 — 6–12 SECONDS: PEOPLE GOING ABOUT THEIR DAY

The camera shifts naturally toward the nearby riverside surroundings without a hard cut. Medieval London residents go about their everyday routines. A woman carries a woven basket, a man transports goods on a wooden handcart, and merchants arrange simple goods near a busy riverside area. A few pedestrians pause to watch the bridge construction.

The people should not behave like extras performing for a movie. They walk, talk, work, carry objects, and interact naturally. Clothing includes rough wool tunics, simple cloaks, linen garments, leather footwear, and historically plausible medieval head coverings. No modern hairstyles, zippers, synthetic fabrics, or contemporary accessories.

The bridge remains partially visible in the background, maintaining visual continuity with the primary subject.

SCENE 3 — 12–18 SECONDS: THE NEARBY LOCALITY

Briefly show the surrounding medieval riverside neighborhood. Narrow, uneven pathways, timber-framed buildings, simple market stalls, wooden carts, and people moving between the river and nearby streets. A boatman guides a small wooden boat along the Thames while another person unloads supplies.

The camera should not linger on the locality. Keep this segment short and observational, providing a glimpse of the world around the construction site. Natural light, realistic atmospheric depth, and accurate medieval material textures. Avoid making the surroundings look like a fantasy kingdom or a modern historical theme park.

SCENE 4 — 18–25 SECONDS: RETURN TO THE CONSTRUCTION

The camera naturally returns its attention to London Bridge. A group of workers carefully moves a large stone block into position. A mason strikes the stone with a hammer and chisel, producing tiny fragments. Another worker signals to his colleagues as a rope is pulled.

Show the physical effort of construction: workers lifting, carrying, balancing, communicating, and adjusting materials. The partially completed stone arches and rough timber supports remain the main visual focus. Use realistic human movement, weight, friction, and gravity. No impossible lifting, floating objects, or magically assembling bridge sections.

A few nearby people watch the construction from a safe distance, while others continue their daily routines without paying much attention.

SCENE 5 — 25–30 SECONDS: A LIVING HISTORICAL MOMENT

End with a naturally framed medium-wide view of the unfinished bridge, the busy riverside, and the people working and walking nearby. The camera remains at human eye level, with slight handheld movement and an imperfect but believable composition.

A worker crosses the foreground carrying a small stone tool, partially obscuring the bridge for a moment. The camera gently adjusts its position, revealing the bridge again. The river, workers, pedestrians, and surrounding medieval buildings create the feeling of an ordinary day unfolding during a major engineering project.

End without a dramatic reveal, artificial camera movement, or modern cinematic transition.

AUTHENTICITY & REALISM REQUIREMENTS:

Make the video look like real historical documentary footage, not an AI-generated reconstruction.
Use natural human facial expressions, realistic skin textures, imperfect teeth, and believable body proportions.
Accurate medieval clothing, tools, building materials, and construction techniques.
The camera operator remains completely invisible. No camera, hands, reflections, or modern equipment.
Natural handheld camera movement, subtle focus adjustments, occasional foreground obstructions, and realistic exposure.
Consistent architectural design and construction progress throughout the video.
Realistic river behavior, wind, dust, stone textures, wood grain, and crowd movement.
No modern objects, electricity, vehicles, contemporary signage, modern buildings, or anachronistic details.
No exaggerated cinematic color grading, fantasy atmosphere, slow motion, time-lapse, artificial lens flares, or background music.
No subtitles, text overlays, watermarks, or visible AI artifacts.
FINAL CREATIVE GOAL:

The viewer should feel as though they have been transported to medieval London for one ordinary day while the iconic stone London Bridge is being built. The bridge construction is the central focus, while nearby people, merchants, workers, boats, and local streets provide a brief but convincing glimpse into authentic medieval life. Every movement, texture, expression, and environmental detail should feel grounded in reality.
```

Comic / Storyboard · Character · Published

**Summary:** 15-second one-shot video prompt of paying the bill at a bar from a first-person perspective, including detailed time storyboards and action specifications.

<img src="covers/2101827048402534496.jpg" alt="Image 1" width="480" />

**Prompt**

```text
# The Bar Proprietress Writes the Bill on the Back of Your Hand | 15 Seconds | One-Shot

【Overall Setup】 First-person bar checkout. Use the face, hairstyle, and body of Preset Character #1 throughout; Character #1 is an adult. Wear only Preset Outfit #3 throughout, including original footwear and accessories. It is forbidden to replace with the face, hairstyle, or clothing of the original actor in the reference video. Only Character #1 appears fully on camera throughout the video. The camera operator is never on camera; only an adult's left hand and part of the forearm are permitted to extend into the frame from the bottom. Narrative causality: You sit before the bar counter and call the proprietress to pay the bill → She closes the ledger and walks over → Grasps your wrist → Writes "36" on the back of your hand → Blows the ink dry → Lifts her eyes waiting for you to see clearly → Concludes in a cool, glamorous posture with a sense of distance.

【Shooting Texture】 9:16 vertical screen, 15 seconds, 720×1280, 30fps. Realistic smartphone native camera, first-person ultra-wide angle, approximately 20–24mm equivalent focal length, low camera angle slightly tilted upward, slight wide-angle edge distortion. The camera is essentially fixed, retaining natural breathing jitter, low-light noise, auto-exposure, and slightly delayed autofocus. Complete one-shot continuous take throughout, no cuts, no transitions, no slow motion, no speed ramping, no cinematic camera movements, no beautification filters, and no excessive color grading.

【Bar Environment】 Real indoor bar setting. On the right, a tall black bar counter and black bar stools; on the left, light brown tables and chairs; in the distance, liquor cabinets and a very small number of blurred patrons. Dark green ceiling, black branch-like decorations, a crystal chandelier at the upper left, small multi-national flags on the back wall, a tilted blue television at the rear right. Dim lighting, authentic yellow-green bar ambient light. Furniture and lighting positions remain fixed throughout.

【Props Lock】 On the bar counter sits a small beige ledger notebook, a black fine-tip gel pen, and a used empty beer glass. The camera operator's left hand enters from the bottom of the frame: first gently tapping the bar counter to call someone, then flipping back-of-hand up for her to write. The wrist is fully visible. The female lead writes only "36", with clear handwriting, slight hand-drawn jitter, ordinary black ink, with realistic specular reflection on the skin. Paper receipts, POS machines, QR codes, bank cards, and mobile payment interfaces are forbidden.

【Strict 15-Second Time Storyboard】
0.00—2.40s: First-person fixed wide-angle. Character #1 stands on the inner side behind the bar counter, body slightly angled leaning against the counter surface, shoulders relaxed, neck elongated, looking down flipping through the ledger notebook, half-lowered eyelids, cold expression, not looking at the camera. The camera operator's left hand reaches in from the bottom of the frame, knuckles lightly tapping the black bar counter twice. Off-screen comes a short, lowered male voice: "Boss, check, please." Her finger flipping through the ledger halts, eyes still cast down, only shifting her gaze from the ledger to your hand tapping the counter.
2.40—4.20s: She closes the ledger notebook, picks up the black pen, and walks around from behind the bar to a spot closer to the camera. Walking slowly: toes landing first, legs crossing naturally, hips showing only a very subtle sway. As she approaches, her left fingertips graze the edge of the bar counter. Shoulders relaxed, neck elongated, gaze moving from your hand toward the camera, eyelids half-lowered casting a cool glance at you, as if confirming it is you who wants to pay. The empty beer glass remains near the lower-left corner of the frame.
4.20—5.80s: She stops on the inside of the bar counter, body slightly angled, one hip lightly leaning against the counter. Her left hand extends out, holding the wrist you stretched in, thumb pressing on the inner wrist with a steady grip, without rubbing or caressing. At the same time, she turns your hand so the back of the hand faces upward, aligned toward herself. Her gaze slides from your eyes to the back of your hand, chin subtly tilting sideways, as if checking whether this piece of "receipt paper" is large enough to write on. Autofocus briefly drifts from her eyes to the back of the hand.
5.80—9.20s: She lowers her head and writes "36" on the back of your hand. When the pen tip touches the skin, there is authentic pausing and gliding; the two digits are clearly written stroke by stroke. While writing, her body remains slightly angled, shoulders lowered, neck elongated, without leaning her entire upper body flat onto the counter. Her left hand holds the wrist the entire time without letting go. Lips gently closed, silent, expression focused and cool. The empty glass and ledger notebook remain in their original positions.
9.20—11.00s: Finished writing. She places the pen back onto the ledger notebook without immediately releasing her grip. Slightly lowering her head, she gently blows a breath onto "36". The airflow is very short, causing a faint reflection to appear on the surface of the ink. After blowing, her thumb still presses your wrist, turning the back of your hand slightly toward the camera so the numbers enter the frame completely. Autofocus locks onto the handwriting, returning to sharp focus after a brief, subtle defocus.
11.00—12.60s: She releases the wrist, fingers leaving the skin and hovering about 5 cm outside the back of the hand. Her gaze slowly lifts from the "36" on the back of the hand to the camera operator's face. Her half-lowered eyelids open slightly, the corners of her mouth holding an extremely faint, almost undetectable curve. She points the pen cap through the air at the numbers to indicate that the bill is right here, the pen cap not touching the skin. Your left hand remains at the bottom of the frame, back of hand facing up, "36" clearly visible.
12.60—15.00s: She slowly straightens up. Body naturally slightly angled, shoulders relaxed and lowered, neck elongated, chin subtly extended sideways, one hip pushed outward, legs staggered front and back. Her left hand rests back on the black bar counter with a supple wrist, right hand pressed against the closed ledger notebook. She first looks down at the not-yet-dry handwriting on the back of your hand, then turns her gaze to the camera, looking straight ahead coldly. The final second freezes: restrained expression, cold and glamorous, with a sense of distance, as if waiting for you to decide whether to order again. The left hand remains at the bottom of the frame with the numbers on the back of the hand visible. The camera holds until 15.00s.

【Progression of Expressions】 Cold indifference while looking down keeping accounts → Confirmation upon hearing the checkout request → Restrained approach as she walks over → Concentration while gripping the wrist and writing → Brief proximity when blowing the ink → Lifting eyes waiting for you to see clearly → Cold, glamorous concluding posture maintaining distance.

【Audio】 No narration, no subtitles. 0.80—1.60s: Two soft taps on the bar counter + off-screen male voice "Boss, check, please." The voice is close, short, and not shouted. Retain quiet bar background music, distant muffled voices, sound of the ledger closing, rustling sound of the pen tip scratching against skin, and the light clatter of the pen being placed back on the surface. 9.20—10.00s: Extremely brief blowing sound. After 12.60s: A very faint exhalation that does not form spoken dialogue. The female lead remains completely silent throughout.

【Negative Prompt】 Forbidden to swap, alter, duplicate, or change the hairstyle of Character #1; forbidden to change the outfit, color, or switch to the original outfit of the reference video for Outfit #3; forbidden: fishtails, fish scales, wet body, glowing eyes, magical particles; forbidden to simultaneously touch the lower lip, side of neck, and hair strands; forbidden: exaggerated waist swaying, lip licking, seductive smiles, clutching fingers without letting go, or pressing the face against the hand; forbidden for the camera operator to appear fully or for a second person to appear; forbidden to write long sentences, English words, profanities, heart shapes, or phone numbers; forbidden for the handwriting to turn into tattoos, glow, or dissolve into the skin; forbidden: receipts, QR codes, mobile payment screens, POS machines; forbidden for the female lead to kiss the back of the hand, use her tongue, or write with alcohol liquid; forbidden: mutated fingers, extra arms, clipping/mesh penetration; forbidden: slow motion, shot cuts, orbiting camera moves, cinematic dolly zooms; forbidden: voiceover narration, subtitles, watermarks, brand logos, and platform UI; forbidden to alter the bar space, lighting, and furniture positions.
```

<a name="prompt-2101689290992542125"></a>

### A young woman in a Korean apartment elevator transforms into an unnatural creature and pursues her panicked neighbor.

Author：[@AIwithkhan](https://x.com/AIwithkhan) · [Source](https://x.com/AIwithkhan/status/2101689290992542125)

Character · Animal / Creature · Published

**Summary:** A young woman in a Korean apartment elevator transforms into an unnatural creature and pursues her panicked neighbor.

<img src="covers/2101689290992542125.jpg" alt="Image 1" width="480" />

**Prompt**

```text
Young Korean woman wearing a fitted blue crop top and white shorts enters an old apartment-building elevator alone; a tired neighbor stands quietly beside her.

The elevator doors close; fluorescent lights flicker while the woman stares at the floor, completely still.

The floor indicator suddenly glitches; she slowly raises her head and looks toward the neighbor with an unsettling blank expression.

Her fingers begin twitching violently; her shoulders jerk as if something is moving beneath her skin.

She suddenly grabs the elevator railing, bends forward unnaturally, then collapses against the wall.

The neighbor steps backward in fear and desperately presses the emergency button.

She becomes completely motionless for a moment, then slowly rises with wide unfocused eyes, pale skin and unnatural posture.

Her head tilts sharply to one side; her hands twitch while her breathing becomes heavy and irregular.

The neighbor tries to force the elevator doors open, but they remain stuck between floors.

The woman suddenly lunges forward and slams against the elevator wall, then turns toward the neighbor with a terrifying expression.

The emergency lights switch to red as the elevator shakes and stops completely.

The neighbor desperately crawls toward the control panel while she slowly approaches from behind.

The elevator suddenly drops a few inches; both stumble as the lights flicker violently.

The doors finally open halfway, revealing a dark apartment hallway outside.

The neighbor squeezes through the opening, but the woman suddenly rushes forward and crawls underneath the closing doors.

The neighbor runs down the hallway as the creature follows with fast, unnatural movements.

A resident opens their apartment door, sees the creature approaching and immediately slams the door shut.

The creature stops, slowly turns toward the camera, then suddenly charges down the hallway.

The hallway lights flicker one by one as the camera backs away toward the stairwell.

The elevator doors remain open behind her, completely empty, while a faint distorted breathing sound comes from inside.

STYLE: Ultra-realistic Korean apartment elevator horror, claustrophobic handheld camera, flickering fluorescent and emergency lighting, realistic skin and facial expressions, unnatural but physically believable body movement, tense pacing, realistic elevator reflections, subtle camera shake, natural motion blur, cinematic but grounded, practical-effects horror, no gore, no blood, no explicit injuries, no subtitles, no watermark, no CGI look.
```

```text
Main Subject: Young Korean woman, 24, naturally attractive, realistic skin, minimal makeup, long dark hair loosely down. Wearing simple casual home clothes while getting ready. Preserve her exact identity, facial features, hairstyle, body proportions and appearance throughout.

Location: Small old Seoul apartment bedroom during a dark, rainy early morning. Open wardrobe, simple wooden dresser, small mirror, folded clothes, canvas shoulder bag and a rain-covered window overlooking blurred neighboring apartment buildings. Steady rain outside.

Lighting & Atmosphere: Cozy, dim blue-hour ambience. Cold blue-gray light from the rainy window mixed with a weak warm bedside lamp. Dark cloudy sky, muted colors, soft shadows and slightly underexposed interior. Quiet, sleepy and intimate rainy-morning atmosphere.

Style: Ultra-realistic early-2000s Sony MiniDV home video filmed by another person holding the camcorder. Completely candid and unstaged, like a family member casually filming her while she gets ready. Natural handheld movement, subtle human shake, imperfect framing, gentle reframing, occasional autofocus hunting, mild exposure shifts, faded colors, soft contrast, authentic DV compression, subtle low-light digital noise and microphone noise. Smooth continuous real-time motion throughout. No stuttering, judder, frame skipping, duplicated frames, stop-motion appearance, excessive motion blur, speed changes or low-frame-rate look. No stabilization or modern cinematic movement.

00:00–00:04: She stands sleepily in front of her open wardrobe, looking through several hanging clothes. She pulls out a simple oversized shirt and holds it against herself while looking toward the mirror.

00:04–00:08: She changes her mind, puts it back and pulls out another shirt. She holds the second one against herself and studies her reflection with a thoughtful expression.

00:08–00:12: She looks between the two shirts, holding one in each hand. She glances toward the camcorder with a tiny uncertain smile, as if silently asking which one she should wear.

00:12–00:16: She places both shirts on the bed and sits down for a moment. She looks toward the rainy window, listening to the rain, then looks back at the clothes.

00:16–00:20: She picks up one shirt again and stands in front of the mirror, briefly holding it against herself from different angles. She turns slightly left and right, checking how it looks.

00:20–00:24: She finally seems satisfied. She hangs the other shirt back in the wardrobe and neatly folds the chosen shirt over her arm.

00:24–00:27: She notices a lightweight jacket hanging nearby, pauses and considers it too. She touches the jacket, then looks outside at the rain through the window.

00:27–00:30: She decides to take the jacket as well. She looks toward the camcorder with a small satisfied smile, picks up her canvas shoulder bag and walks out of the bedroom. The camera casually follows her for a couple of steps before cutting.

Audio: Natural location sound only—steady rain against the window, clothes rustling, wardrobe doors sliding, soft footsteps, fabric movement, faint distant traffic and quiet apartment ambience. No music, narration or added sound effects.

Goal: Feel like an ordinary 30-second piece of early-2000s family footage rather than a structured fashion video. The story is simply her slowly deciding what to wear on a rainy morning. Small pauses, indecision and casual movements should make it feel spontaneous, intimate and authentic.

Motion quality: Keep every action smooth, continuous and physically realistic. Allow enough time for each movement to complete naturally. Avoid rapid cuts or compressed actions. The handheld camera should gently drift and reframe like a real person casually filming. No stuttering, judder, frame skipping, duplicated frames or low-frame-rate appearance. The vintage look should come entirely from authentic MiniDV characteristics, not from choppy motion.
```

```text
QUALITY LOCK (highest priority): Look like a real modern AAA 3D game captured in-engine, not an AI video or film trailer. Native 4K Unreal Engine 5 gameplay recording. Nanite geometry, Lumen GI, high-frequency texture detail, crisp edges in motion, stable temporal anti-aliasing, deep focus. Ultra-sharp sandstone grain, marble veining, tile mosaics, fabric weave, leather pores, metal edge highlights, individual hair strands. No softness, no waxy/plastic skin, no painterly look, no smearing, no low-res textures, no shallow cinematic DOF that melts background detail. HUD razor-sharp. 60fps feel.
Environment: Enormous ancient Persian palace-city on a cliff above a golden desert. Monumental sandstone and marble towers, collapsed bridges between minarets, giant statues of winged bulls and kings half-buried in sand, waterfalls from fractured aqueducts into hanging gardens, vines in courtyards, golden time-machinery in the ruins. Visual style: Photorealistic UE5 AAA third-person gameplay presentation.
```
